To overcome these limitations, we propose "Token Factory", a framework designed to transform traditional signals into "soft tokens" that can be directly processed by LRMs. This approach enables efficient integration and compression of heterogeneous input features, preventing prompt length explosion while enhancing model performance. We detail the architecture of Token Factory and present experimental results validating its effectiveness in a production-scale recommendation environment.

</details>


### [149] [SAFE-Cascade: Cost-Adaptive Vision-Language Routing for Chart Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19646)
*Ayush Dwivedi,Qixin Wang,Ashvi Soni,Ruoteng Wang,Han Li,Animesh Mahapatra,Neeraj Agrawal,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SAFE-Cascade 是一款面向图表问答的成本自适应交互系统，通过 OCR 和路由机制决定是否调用多模态模型，在保持高性能的同时降低了推理开销并提高了透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对图表问答任务，直接对所有查询调用昂贵且耗时的多模态大模型（VLM）存在资源浪费，且缺乏透明度。

Method: 提出一种级联系统：先通过 OCR 提取文本，利用轻量级文本大模型（GPT-4o-mini）生成初步答案，随后由训练好的随机森林路由模型决定是否需要调用视觉大模型（Gemini-1.5-flash）进行复杂推理。

Result: 在 ChartQA 数据集上，该系统实现了 69.1% 的准确率（与 100% 调用 VLM 的基线 67.7% 持平），同时将 VLM 调用次数减少了 26.9%，预估成本降低了 9.3%。

Conclusion: SAFE-Cascade 证明了通过选择性模态路由，可以在几乎不损失准确性的前提下显著降低图表问答系统的计算成本，并提升了系统的透明度和可调性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are powerful for chart question answering, but invoking a VLM for every query can be unnecessarily expensive when many questions are answerable from OCR text and lightweight language reasoning. We demonstrate SAFE-Cascade, an interactive system for cost-adaptive chart question answering. Given a chart image and a natural-language question, SAFE-Cascade first extracts chart text with OCR, obtains a provisional answer from a text-only language model, and then uses a learned router to decide whether to accept the text answer or escalate to a VLM. The demo exposes this decision process to users: OCR evidence, text-only answer, routing probability, escalation decision, final answer, estimated cost, and estimated latency are shown side by side. SAFE-Cascade is designed as a transparent interface for understanding when visual grounding is actually needed. Users can upload or select charts, ask questions, inspect the evidence used by each pathway, compare text-only and VLM answers, and adjust the escalation threshold to explore the accuracy-cost frontier. The system is implemented with Azure Document Intelligence for OCR, gpt-5-mini as the text-only model, gemini-2.5-flash-image as the VLM, and a Random Forest router trained on inference-time features. On a held-out ChartQA test split of 375 examples from a 2,500-example experiment, SAFE-Cascade achieves 69.1% unified accuracy with 73.1% VLM invocation, compared with 67.7% accuracy and 100% VLM invocation for the full-VLM baseline. The observed +1.4 percentage-point difference is statistically uncertain, so we interpret SAFE-Cascade as matching full-VLM performance while reducing VLM calls by 26.9% and estimated cost by 9.3%. The demonstration shows how selective modality routing can make multimodal knowledge systems more transparent, tunable, and cost-aware.

</details>


### [150] [Closing the Calibration Gap in Semantic Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19719)
*Aditeya Baral,Radoslav Ralev,Iliya Sotirov Zhechev,Srijith Rajamohan,Jen Agarwal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对语义缓存评估中 PR-AUC 指标的失准问题，本文提出了 P-CHR AUC 和 CRR 等更贴合部署需求的指标，指出模型评估应聚焦于校准而非仅关注排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义缓存方案主要使用 PR-AUC 作为评估标准，但该指标忽略了固定阈值下的可用性，导致高 PR-AUC 的模型在实际部署中表现往往不佳。

Method: 1. 引入了 P-CHR AUC（精度-缓存命中率曲线下面积）来衡量缓存利用率下的精度；2. 提出了 CRR（校准保持率）以评估离线排序质量在部署中的存留情况；3. 将离线与部署间的操作差距分解为可恢复的校准分量和不可约的结构分量。

Result: 实验表明，离线与部署性能之间的差距主要由训练目标决定，而非数据规模；现有的后验校准方法只能部分弥补这一差距。

Conclusion: 语义缓存的模型选择本质上是一个校准问题而非排序问题。使用 PR-AUC 进行模型评估会导致部署效果不佳，建议采用 P-CHR AUC 和 CRR 等新指标来衡量实际部署性能。

Abstract: Semantic caching cuts LLM inference costs by serving a cached response to semantically similar queries. Standard practice evaluates these systems using PR-AUC, a metric that only measures how well scores rank and ignores whether they are usable at a fixed threshold. We show this mismatch leads to systematically poor deployment choices, as models with the highest PR-AUC are often the worst in operation. We introduce Precision-Cache Hit Ratio (P-CHR) AUC, a cache-aware metric that measures precision across cache utilization levels, and Calibration Retention Rate (CRR), which captures how much offline ranking quality survives at deployment. We decompose the operational gap between offline and deployed quality into a recoverable calibration component and an irreducible structural component fixed by the dataset's positive rate. Our experiments show that the calibration gap is governed by the training objective rather than data scale, and post-hoc calibration only partially closes it. Ultimately, model selection for semantic caching is a calibration problem, not a ranking one, and measuring it is the first step to closing the gap.

</details>


### [151] [Stellar: Scalable Multimodal Document Retrieval for Natural Language Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19960)
*Yuxiang Guo,Zhonghao Hu,Yuren Mao,Yuhang Liu,Congcong Ge,Xiaolu Zhang,Jun Zhou,Yunjun Gao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Stellar 是一种可扩展的多模态检索框架，通过词汇过滤过滤候选文档，并动态磁盘加载 token 嵌入，在显著降低内存占用和延迟的同时保持了检索准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态检索方法采用多向量后交互（late interaction）机制，虽然精度高，但存在巨大的内存消耗，阻碍了大规模场景下的实际部署。

Method: 提出 Stellar 框架：1. LRF：利用 MLLM 作为稀疏编码器生成词汇表示，进行初步过滤以缩减候选集；2. DLI：利用平衡聚类算法设计磁盘存储布局，并基于代价模型动态加载必要的 token 嵌入。

Result: 在四个真实数据集及一个大规模新数据集上，Stellar 在保持检索效果的同时，将内存消耗和查询延迟降低了 1 到 2 个数量级。

Conclusion: Stellar 通过引入基于词汇表示的过滤（LRF）和高效的磁盘缓存机制（DLI），成功解决了多模态文档检索中多向量表示带来的内存消耗问题，实现了高性能与高扩展性的平衡。

Abstract: Multimodal document retrieval--selecting the most relevant multimodal document from a large corpus to answer a natural language query--plays an essential role in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems. State-of-the-art methods represent each document and query with multiple token-level embeddings and use late interaction to achieve high effectiveness. However, such multi-vector representations incur substantial memory overhead during retrieval, leading to poor scalability and hindering real-world deployment. In this paper, we present Stellar, a scalable multimodal document retrieval framework that stores token-level document embeddings on disk and loads only a small set of candidate embeddings into memory for late interaction. Stellar comprises two key components: (i) Lexical Representation-based Filtering (LRF), which fine-tunes a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a sparse encoder to produce high-quality lexical representations, enabling efficient and effective document filtering to significantly reduce the candidate set; (ii) Efficient Disk-backed Late Interaction (DLI), which designs an on-disk token embedding storage layout guided by a balanced clustering algorithm, and dynamically loads only the necessary token embeddings into memory using a simple yet effective cost model. Extensive experiments on four real-world benchmarks and a newly presented large-scale dataset demonstrate that Stellar reduces memory overhead and query latency by 1-2 orders of magnitude compared to existing methods without compromising retrieval effectiveness.

</details>


### [152] [PACMS: Submodular Context Selection as a Pluggable Engine for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20047)
*Manu Ghulyani,Arunabh Singh,Karan Bharadwaj,Ankit Nath,Suranjan Goswami*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 现有LLM代理的上下文管理普遍依赖“最近优先”截断，导致关键历史信息丢失。本文呼吁开发一种能根据查询相关性，对全部历史对话、记忆和工具输出进行统一筛选与管理的机制，以解决长上下文场景下的记忆失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在处理长期对话或工具调用时，受限于上下文窗口限制。现有的“最近优先（recency truncation）”截断策略不仅丢失重要历史信息，还保留了大量冗余内容，无法满足高效记忆和长逻辑推理的需求。

Method: 本文分析指出了现有上下文管理方法的局限性，指出应从“外部知识检索”或“盲目文本压缩”转向基于即时相关性的上下文联合仲裁机制。

Result: 通过对比分析，得出结论：现有的RAG和压缩技术均无法在“组装阶段”对现有上下文进行有效的相关性裁决，无法实现上下文内容的优化配置。

Conclusion: 目前的上下文管理方案（如最近优先截断）存在“主题盲目性”缺陷，即无法根据当前查询的关联性保留关键信息。亟需一种能将对话记录、记忆条目与工具输出视为统一池，并基于即时查询相关性进行动态筛选的机制。

Abstract: Conversational and tool-using LLM agents operate over a context window that fills from several directions simultaneously. As a session proceeds, the agent accumulates user and assistant turns, entries drawn from a persistent memory store, and often largest of all, the verbatim outputs of tool calls such as file reads, search results, and API responses. Once the cumulative context exceeds the model's token budget, the framework must decide what to keep.
  The prevailing mechanism is recency truncation, sometimes paired with periodic summarization. This is topic-blind: a fact established early in a session is discarded simply because it is old, even when the current user query is about exactly that fact; conversely, verbose but irrelevant recent material is retained. Agents that must recall information across many turns, the defining case for memory, are precisely where recency truncation fails.
  Existing alternatives sit outside the agent's assembly step. Retrieval augmented generation fetches external documents into the prompt but does not arbitrate the agent's \emph{already-present} pooled context. Context-compression methods reduce token count by rewriting or pruning text, but operate query-blind and lossily. Neither treats memory entries, conversation turns, and tool outputs as a single candidate pool to be selected from by relevance at the moment the prompt is assembled.

</details>


### [153] [Generative Engine Optimization at Scale: Measuring Brand Visibility Across AI Search Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20065)
*Pratyush Kumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了生成式引擎优化（GEO），发现AI搜索引擎的品牌可见性呈现明显的阶梯式差异，并识别出官网内容和排行榜文章是提升AI引用率的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着用户从传统搜索引擎转向ChatGPT、Claude等生成式AI，原有的SEO策略已不足以覆盖AI引擎。品牌需要理解如何优化其在AI生成答案中的展示和推荐，即生成式引擎优化（GEO）。

Method: 利用Ranqo平台，在2026年3月至5月期间，针对100多个品牌的10万多条Prompt响应进行了大规模分析，以衡量不同类型品牌的可见性轨迹、引用来源偏好及内容形式权重。

Result: 1. 存在显著的品牌地位阶梯：全球知名品牌可见性达73%，中型品牌44%，小型/利基品牌仅11%。2. 引用来源高度集中于企业官网（78%），其次是YouTube等平台。3. 排行榜类清单（Listicles）是最高效的内容形式。4. 品牌情感倾向是高度波动的指标。

Conclusion: AI品牌可见性（GEO）是可以测量的，且受品牌成熟度影响巨大。研究提出了七项初步GEO协议，为未来通过策略优化提升AI可见性奠定了基础。

Abstract: People increasingly get answers straight from AI search engines like ChatGPT, Claude, Perplexity, and Gemini rather than scrolling search results. Brands that once focused on search engine optimization (SEO) must now optimize for how these engines represent, cite, and recommend them -- a shift variously called Generative Engine Optimization (GEO), Answer Engine Optimization (AEO), and AI Search Visibility. We treat AEO and AI Visibility as part of GEO, and study how to measure brand visibility across AI engines: what they value when they cite a brand, which sources they rely on, and what content large language models surface. The hard case is everyone outside the already-authoritative top brands -- SMEs, D2C brands, creators, and early-stage startups.
  We analyze 100K+ prompt responses across 100+ brands tracked on Ranqo between March and May 2026. First visibility runs form a clear three-tier brand-stature ladder: global household names (e.g., Stripe, Nike) appear in 73% of relevant AI answers on their first run; established mid-market and regional brands (e.g., Olipop, Klaviyo) in 44%; niche and small brands in just 11% -- about 30 percentage points per step. When engines cite sources, about 78% go to corporate websites; among non-corporate sources YouTube leads, ahead of Reddit, editorial media, and Wikipedia. The highest-leverage page is the ranked "best-of" listicle, the most-cited content format at about 21% of all citations. Sentiment is the unstable signal: whether a brand is framed positively or negatively flips about 6.7 times more often than whether it is mentioned at all.
  These findings provide a first large-scale baseline for measuring GEO: AI brand visibility can be measured, differs by platform, and varies strongly by brand maturity. We close by proposing seven v1.1 protocols to test whether specific recommendations can causally improve AI visibility.

</details>


### [154] [ScholarQuest: A Taxonomy-Guided Benchmark for Agentic Academic Paper Search in Open Literature Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20235)
*Tingyue Pan,Mingyue Cheng,Daoyu Wang,Yitong Zhou,Jie Ouyang,Qi Liu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个大规模、分类引导的学术论文搜索基准ScholarQuest，旨在系统性评估并提升LLM智能体在真实学术场景下的文献检索能力与意图理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学术文献检索基准无法有效评估现实环境下智能体驱动的迭代文献探索能力。

Method: 构建了大规模、分类引导的基准测试集ScholarQuest，涵盖1,000多个计算机科学主题和四种研究意图；提供可扩展的答案构建机制及共享检索后端ScholarBase。

Result: 实验表明，智能体方法优于单次检索基线，但在Recall@100和Recall@All指标上仍表现较低，且通过多维度分析揭示了智能体在效率、鲁棒性和失败模式上的具体短板。

Conclusion: 现有的基于LLM的学术搜索智能体表现仍有很大提升空间（Recall@100仅为0.314），ScholarQuest通过多维评估为该领域提供了基准和改进方向。

Abstract: Academic paper search is a core step in scientific research, and LLM-based search agents are emerging as a promising paradigm for iterative, intent-driven literature exploration. However, existing benchmarks are insufficient for systematically evaluating agentic academic search under realistic open literature environments. We propose ScholarQuest, a large-scale, taxonomy-guided benchmark for agentic academic paper search. ScholarQuest is constructed from over 1,000 computer science topics and four representative research intents, including method-oriented, setting-anchored, comparison-based, and scope-controlled queries. It further provides scalable answer construction and a shared retrieval backend ScholarBase for reproducible evaluation. Benchmarking results show that agentic methods outperform single-shot retrieval baselines, yet the best-performing agent only achieves 0.314 Recall@100 and 0.355 Recall@All, indicating substantial room for improvement. In addition, analyses of search efficiency, intent-level robustness, and failure cases further highlight the benchmark's ability to provide multi-dimensional evaluation signals for academic paper search agents.

</details>


### [155] [ELVA: Exploring Ranking-Driven Universal Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20280)
*Yuhan Liu,Pei Fu,Hang Li,Yukun Qi,Chao Jiang,Jingwen Fu,Zhen Liu,Bin Qin,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan,Jingmin Xin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对多模态检索中模型忽略查询细粒度信息（粒度盲点）的问题，论文提出了ELVA框架，通过无需显式标签的规则强化学习，优化负样本排序与特征区分，显著提升了模型在复杂查询场景下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于对比学习的方法在处理复杂查询时存在“粒度盲点”，即将样本简单划分为正负对，忽略了负样本之间信息含量的差异，导致模型无法捕捉查询中的关键细粒度信息。

Method: 提出了一种基于规则的强化学习框架（ELVA），通过扩展带有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），利用规则奖励机制来联合优化负样本排序并扩大正负样本间的相似度差距，从而引导MLLMs学习细粒度的信息。

Result: ELVA在多个标准检索任务上达到SOTA水平，并在专门针对细粒度查询场景设计的MRBench基准上实现了13.1%的性能提升。

Conclusion: ELVA通过引入基于规则的强化学习框架，成功解决了通用多模态检索中的“粒度盲点（Grain Blindness）”问题，并在标准测试集及自建的MRBench基准上取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) via contrastive learning has become a mainstream paradigm for improving the performance of Universal Multimodal Retrieval (UMR). However, previous works have ignored the grain blindness when adapting the contrastive paradigm into retrieval tasks. Grain blindness refers to the tendency of the model to overlook grain-level information contained in the query, which is crucial for effectively handling complex queries. This stems from contrastive learning treating samples as a binary classification (positive/negative), while ignoring the different information carried by each negative sample. To address this, we argue that negatives should be treated differently according to their similarity to the positive sample, enabling the model to learn distinct grain information from each negative. In this paper, we introduce a simple but effective framework, called ELVA, a novel rule-based RL framework that mitigates grain blindness through ranking-driven MLLMs. 1) Instead of relying on reward models, we extend Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards
  (RLVR) to retrieval tasks, allowing the model to explore new ranking behaviors without explicit ranking labels. 2) By utilizing rule-based rewards, our approach jointly optimizes the ranking of negative samples while enlarging the similarity gap between positive and negative. To more precisely measure grain blindness, we further introduce MRBench, a new benchmark specifically designed for multi-grain query scenarios. ELVA achieves state-of-the-art results across standard retrieval benchmarks, and its notable 13.1% improvement on MRBench further demonstrates its effectiveness in alleviating grain blindness.

</details>


### [156] [Structuring and Tokenizing Distributed User Interest Context for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20554)
*Ruizhong Qiu,Yinglong Xia,Dongqi Fu,Hanqing Zeng,Ren Chen,Xiangjun Fan,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: G2Rec是一个旨在解决工业级生成式推荐中用户兴趣上下文建模难点的新型框架，它通过融合全局图协同建模与语义标记化技术，实现了无需标签下的高质量用户兴趣原型捕获，提升了推荐表现与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式推荐方法在处理复杂的交互行为与语义上下文时存在局限性：基于图的方法难以扩展或仅能利用局部信息；基于语义标记化的方法依赖启发式规则且缺乏显式监督，导致表示不准确。

Method: 提出了G2Rec框架，该框架将全局图协同交互建模与语义标记化方法进行有机结合，通过捕获整体且具有语义基础的用户兴趣原型，解决了现有方法在扩展性和语义表达方面的不足。

Result: 通过在线部署与多项公开数据集测试，结果表明G2Rec优于现有的生成式推荐算法，能够提供更全面、准确的用户行为上下文建模。

Conclusion: G2Rec是一个适用于工业级生成式推荐的高效框架，通过统一全局图模型与语义标记化，能够有效地建模用户兴趣上下文，在保持可扩展性的同时显著提升了推荐性能。

Abstract: Generative recommendation is an emerging paradigm that has shown promise in industrial recommendation systems, aiming to predict users' next interactions from their historical behaviors. At the core of generative recommendation lies item tokenization, which bridges item semantics and recommendation models. However, existing methods often struggle to effectively organize and inject complex user-behavioral and item-semantic contexts into recommendation models simultaneously. On the one hand, existing graph-based integration methods, such as graph serialization and graph neural networks, either suffer from scalability issues or exploit only local graph information. On the other hand, existing semantic tokenization methods typically rely on heuristics and lack explicit supervision signals, which may lead to inaccurate or suboptimal semantic representations. To address these limitations in user interest context modeling, we propose G2Rec, a scalable framework that unifies holistic graph-based user co-engagement modeling with semantic tokenization for industrial-scale generative recommendation. Overall, G2Rec enables recommendation models to capture holistic and semantically grounded user interest prototypes without requiring ground-truth user interests, thereby providing more comprehensive and accurate modeling of user behavior contexts in industrial sequential recommendation. Online deployment across product surfaces and extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate the superiority of G2Rec over existing methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [A Topos-Theoretic Interpretation of Blockchain Systems: Sheaves of Consensus and the Logic of Decentralized Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19519)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对传统区块链形式化模型（有限状态机）难以描述去中心化共识的缺陷，本文主张采用“拓扑理论”（Topos Theory）——即层范畴理论，作为刻画区块链局部一致性与全局真值构建的核心数学语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的区块链及智能合约形式化模型（如有限状态机）忽视了共识机制这一核心问题，将其归为模型之外的实现细节，无法忠实描述去中心化环境下的计算现象。

Method: 提出利用拓扑理论（Topos Theory，即层范畴理论）作为构建区块链共识模型的数学框架，以取代传统的有限状态机（FSM）模型。

Result: 建立了一种新的理论视角，将区块链的计算现象从简单的状态迁移提升至基于局部一致性与全局真值构建的数学范畴，为共识机制的建模提供了理论支撑。

Conclusion: 拓扑理论（Topos Theory）是描述区块链系统的原生数学语言，它通过层（sheaves）的范畴理论，能够从数学上严谨地刻画从局部共识到全局真值构建的过程。

Abstract: The predominant formal models for blockchain systems, particularly smart contracts, have largely been drawn from the classical theory of computation, with the finite state machine (FSM) or labeled transition system serving as the primary conceptual tool. However, the FSM relegates the most difficult and novel aspect of a blockchain -- the achievement of consensus in a decentralized environment -- to a complex, often messy, implementation detail that lies outside the formal model itself. But the process of consensus is not an ancillary feature; it is the very essence of the computational phenomenon. To model it faithfully, a new mathematical language is required. The central thesis of this work is that topos theory, the theory of categories of sheaves, provides the native mathematical language for systems defined by local consistency and the construction of global truth.

</details>


### [158] [The Sheaf Laplacian: A Topological Framework for Data Fusion and Consensus in Distributed Sensing Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19529)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 传统图模型已不足以描述现代分布式系统的复杂性；本文提出引入层理论（Sheaf Theory），利用层拉普拉斯算子实现多模态数据融合与网络共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于简单图（Simple Graph）的网络模型无法有效捕捉现代分布式系统中的异构性、高维/多模态数据特征以及复杂的上下文相关动态关系。

Method: 引入并应用数学中的层理论（Sheaf Theory），并提出使用层拉普拉斯算子（Sheaf Laplacian）作为分布式传感环境中处理多模态数据和达成共识的核心工具。

Result: 验证了层理论在应对复杂分布式系统挑战方面的适用性，并证明了层拉普拉斯算子在数据融合和网络共识任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 层理论（Sheaf Theory）为现代复杂分布式系统的建模和分析提供了一种比传统图模型更具表现力和数学严谨性的新范式，其中层拉普拉斯算子是实现复杂数据融合与协同一致性的有效机制。

Abstract: We argue here that traditional network models, which are overwhelmingly based on the mathematical construct of a simple graph, are fundamentally insufficient for capturing the complexity of modern distributed systems. Such systems are characterized by heterogeneous agents with diverse capabilities, high-dimensional and multi-modal data streams, and intricate, context-dependent relationships that cannot be adequately described by a simple connection or a scalar weight. The limitations of these classical models necessitate a new mathematical language, one with far greater expressive power. We have found that sheaf theory provides us with such a language. Moreover, we show that the sheaf Laplacian is a suitable mechanism for data fusion and establishing consensus within distributed sensing networks.

</details>


### [159] [SAC: Disaggregated KV Cache System for Sparse Attention LLMs with CXL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19746)
*Ruiyang Ma,Teng Ma,Junru Li,Hantian Zha,Xuchun Shang,Qingda Hu,Zheng Liu,Xinjun Yang,Tao Ma,Guojie Luo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对稀疏注意力模型在长上下文推理中存在的 KV 缓存远程获取效率问题，SAC 利用 CXL 的按需细粒度加载机制，显著提升了吞吐量并降低了延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 上下文长度的增加，推理瓶颈从计算转向内存。现有的基于 RDMA 的分布式内存系统在面对稀疏注意力模型时效率低下，因为它们强制加载整个 KV 缓存，导致带宽浪费和本地内存冗余。

Method: 提出了一种名为 SAC 的高效解耦 KV 缓存系统，利用 CXL 高速互联技术的缓存行（cache-line）细粒度内存获取特性，实现按需获取稀疏注意力计算中所需的 top-k KV 条目，无需一次性加载完整 KV 缓存。

Result: 在 DeepSeek-V3.2 和 SGLang 框架下的实验表明，与基于 RDMA 的基线相比，SAC 的系统吞吐量提高了 2.1 倍，首字延迟（TTFT）降低了 9.7 倍，每 token 延迟（TBT）降低了 1.8 倍。

Conclusion: CXL 架构在处理稀疏注意力模型时，优于传统的基于 RDMA 的解耦内存方案，是未来长上下文推理系统的关键基础设施。

Abstract: The scaling of LLMs toward long-context inference has shifted the primary serving system bottleneck from computation to memory capacity. Traditional solutions for dense attention models rely on RDMA-based disaggregated memory pools, which perform coarse-grained fetching of the entire prefix KV cache from remote storage to local memory before decoding. However, this approach is fundamentally inefficient for emerging sparse attention models. While only a small fraction of KV entries are active during decoding, these systems still fetch the full KV cache locally, leading to severe transmission bottlenecks and local memory wastage. To address this, we propose SAC, the first efficient disaggregated KV cache system optimized for sparse attention models. By leveraging the low-latency, cache-line granularity load/store semantics of Compute Express Link (CXL), SAC fetches only the required top-k KV entries on demand during inference. Evaluations on DeepSeek-V3.2 using SGLang show that SAC achieves 2.1x higher throughput, 9.7x lower TTFT, and 1.8x lower TBT compared to RDMA-based baselines, establishing CXL-based disaggregation as the superior infrastructure for emerging sparse attention models.

</details>


### [160] [Certified Euclidean-Residue Minimal-Alignment Switch Decompositions for Three Edge-Disjoint Hamiltonian Cycles in Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19832)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于非互素艾森斯坦-雅可比（EJ）网络的本地切换演算方法，通过最少的切换操作实现了哈密顿分解，并构建了基于代数证书的符号化证明框架，摒弃了传统的计算枚举验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 EJ 网络哈密顿分解主要依赖于矩形表示和交换调度，方法复杂。作者旨在通过一种更直观的几何方法，为非互素（$d>1$）情形下的 EJ 网络提供一种更为紧凑、公式化且切换次数最少的分解方案。

Method: 引入了一种基于自然凯莱几何（Cayley geometry）的本地切换演算方法。通过在组件中进行 $d-1$ 次最小化切换构建前两个哈密顿圈，并利用补图构建第三个循环。证明过程分解为组件标签坍缩、锚点消除、提升切换代表元的非碰撞性以及连通补图关联四个核心部分。

Result: 提出了一种非迭代的、公式驱动的 EJ 网络哈密顿分解方案。通过代数证书证明了该方法在特定参数范围下的有效性，避免了依赖有限枚举或计算验证来证明无限族定理。

Conclusion: 该论文提供了一种针对非互素艾森斯坦-雅可比（EJ）网络的高效、基于公式的最小切换哈密顿分解算法，证明了该方法在特定代数参数范围内有效，并强调了纯符号化推导而非计算验证的重要性。

Abstract: Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six quotient-lattice interconnection networks. For a generator $α=a+bρ$, let $N=a^2+ab+b^2$ and $d=\gcd(a,b)$. If $d=1$, the three natural unit directions already give three edge-disjoint Hamiltonian cycles. If $d>1$, each unit direction splits into $d$ cycles and the EDHC problem becomes a cycle-splicing problem. Existing non-coprime EJ decompositions prove existence by using a rectangular representation and exchange schedules. This paper develops a different, local switch calculus in the natural Cayley geometry. The first two Hamiltonian cycles are built using the minimum possible $d-1$ intercomponent switches each, and the third factor is obtained as the unused edge complement. The contribution is deliberately not a new existence theorem for all non-coprime EJ networks; rather, it is a compact, formula-driven, minimal-switch decomposition for Euclidean-residue families whose complement incidence is proved symbolically. The proof separates four ingredients: component-label collapse, anchor cancellation, noncollision of lifted switch representatives, and connected complement incidence. No infinite-family theorem in this manuscript is proved by finite witnesses or by computational enumeration. The theorem scope is stated for the parameter ranges where an algebraic complement-incidence certificate is written down. Tables and CSV data are used only to verify and reproduce the formulas, never as proof of an infinite-family theorem.

</details>


### [161] [Fault-Tolerant Shared-Relay Communication in Circulant Interconnection Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19833)
*Bader Albader,Galal Hassan,Mohamed R. Al-Mulla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有向循环网络中两跳路径的中继节点冗余问题，提出了一个系统化的网络设计框架。研究发现，传统的区间生成器容错性较差，而通过优化生成器选择，可以在较小的度数开销下实现接近理论上限的故障容错能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环互联网络具有对称寻址和均匀局部连通性的优点，但需解决在受限度数 budget 下，如何量化和实现有向循环网络中“两跳基元”的容错性（即共享中继节点的多重性 R(n,m)），以应对故障场景。

Method: 基于循环差分重数条件（cyclic difference-multiplicity condition），构建了一个网络设计框架，涵盖了度数与冗余度指标分析、继电器表预处理与查找算法、对抗性与随机故障保证，并通过对526,539个生成器集的大规模基准测试和微基准研究验证了设计方案。

Result: 定义并量化了 R(n,m) 和 D_f(n) 指标，证明了区间循环图在容错性上的结构局限性，并提出了能显著优于标准设计的优化生成器方案。在广泛的实验中，优化的设计方案表现出极高的可靠性和负载均衡能力。

Conclusion: 生成器的选择对有向循环网络中继节点的容错性至关重要。优化的阈值设计方案可以将容错性能提升至接近理论下界（1.16-1.63倍），而传统的区间生成器在即使增加度数的情况下也可能存在结构性的低容错缺陷。

Abstract: Circulant interconnection networks provide symmetric addressing, compact generator descriptions, and uniform local connectivity. This paper maps a degree--redundancy landscape for a fault-tolerant two-hop primitive in directed circulants: given $n$ nodes and degree budget $m$, how large can the worst-case shared-relay multiplicity $R(n,m)$ be? A node is a shared relay for an ordered terminal pair if it has outgoing links to both terminals; an $f$-relay-fault-tolerant circulant requires at least $f+1$ such relays for every pair. The underlying feasibility condition is a cyclic difference-multiplicity condition, which we use as a mathematical tool rather than claim as a new object. The contribution is the network-design framework around this tool: the parameters $R(n,m)$ and $D_f(n)$, a negative theorem for interval circulants, relay-table preprocessing and lookup algorithms, adversarial and random failure guarantees, load-balance scope, certified upper-bound interpretation of heuristic designs, exact small-$n$ calibration, a software lookup-versus-search microbenchmark, and a reproducible study of 526,539 generator sets. The results show that generator choice critically determines worst-case relay survivability: optimized threshold designs achieve $f$-relay-fault tolerance within about $1.16$--$1.63$ of the counting lower bound, while standard interval generators can fail structurally even at much larger degrees.

</details>


### [162] [Multi-Orientation Edge-Minimum Repair for Non-Redundant Fault-Tolerant Broadcasting in Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19834)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了 EJ-MOEM 算法，用于解决密集 Eisenstein--Jacobi 网络中的非冗余广播树备份与修复，通过理论证明与大规模数值模拟，验证了该方案在单/双故障下的高效修复能力与深度约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决密集 Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) 网络在拓扑中面对故障时的非冗余、高效广播修复问题，旨在确保即使存在一个或两个故障节点时，仍能维持有效的广播生成树结构。

Method: 提出了 EJ-MOEM（多方向边最小修复）算法：通过评估六边形广播树方向、识别故障相关候选项、收缩故障剪枝后的树组件，并利用外部跨组件修复边重建根生成树。同时利用三带表示、扇区后缀附加引理等几何与代数工具进行了理论证明。

Result: 验证了 EJ-MOEM 算法在 $t$ 直径高达 200 的网络中实现了 100% 的成功率。证明了对于包含 $c$ 个健康组件的图，该方法产生的修复边数量是必要且充分的，并确立了单故障和双故障情形下修复树的最大深度上限。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的 EJ-MOEM 算法实现了密集 EJ 网络中的非冗余一对多广播修复。证明了在特定条件下，仅需 $c-1$ 条外部修复边即可重构生成树；理论证明和大规模仿真均证实了其在单故障和双故障场景下的修复深度边界（分别为 $t+1$ 和 $t+2$）。

Abstract: Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six algebraic interconnection networks whose finite quotient geometry is naturally represented by a hexagonal axial-coordinate ball. This paper studies non-redundant one-to-all broadcast repair in the dense EJ network generated by $α=(t+1)+tω$, where $t$ is the network diameter. We propose EJ-MOEM, a multi-orientation edge-minimum repair method that evaluates a constant-size family of hexagonal broadcast-tree orientations, selects a fault-aware candidate, contracts the fault-pruned tree into healthy components, and reconnects these components using external component-crossing repair edges. The resulting structure is a rooted spanning tree of the healthy subgraph: every healthy node receives the message exactly once, no faulty node is used, and the original healthy tree components are preserved. We prove that, for a chosen orientation whose fault-pruned component graph is connected, exactly $c-1$ external repair edges are necessary and sufficient, where $c$ is the number of healthy components. We also prove a depth-certificate theorem for EJ coordinate-reduction trees: every one-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+1$, and every two-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+2$. The proof uses the three-strip representation of EJ hexagons, a sector-suffix attachment lemma, a non-adjacent-sector separation lemma, and a six-direction shielding classification for paired cuts. Extended validation includes exhaustive one- and two-fault enumeration for $t=2,\ldots,12,14,16,18$ (up to $N=1027$ and 525,825 two-fault placements at $t=18$), structured theorem-critical tests through $t=30$, and large random tests through $t=200$, all with 100\% success and no violation of the theorem.

</details>


### [163] [EVM Workloads in the Wild: Evidence for Multi-Dimensional Gas Metering, State Growth, Delayed Execution, and Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19869)
*Lioba Heimbach,Kushal Babel,Jason Milionis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对以太坊L1和Base L2进行大规模追踪分析，证明了单一Gas计费模型难以应对复杂的资源需求与状态敏感性。实际数据表明，计算、存储及状态增长的资源配比极不稳定，执行结果高度依赖于当前状态，这直接导致了多维Gas定价与状态增长显式定价的迫切需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前EVM兼容链的Gas计费模型基于“执行条件稳定”的假设（即资源配置恒定、状态漂移不影响执行结果）。研究旨在实证分析该假设在实际应用中失效的程度，并揭示其对交易预测和用户体验的影响。

Method: 采用追踪级度量研究（Trace-level measurement study），对2025年以太坊L1和Base L2的交易进行全样本分析（每日采样3,000个区块）。将交易分解为操作码级Gas成本、内在Gas、退款及持久化状态增量。通过在不同旧状态上重放交易来测定执行结果对状态的依赖性（State sensitivity）。

Result: 1. 资源成本结构极不稳定：L1与L2资源占比差异巨大，且网络参数调整（如Gas Limit调整）会改变资源配比；2. 状态敏感性显著：Base上46%的交易受历史状态影响导致Gas估计偏离；3. 状态增长成本被低估：Base在2025年产生的持久化状态增长（456 GB）远超以太坊（38 GB），当前的交易Gas费未能有效覆盖此长久成本。

Conclusion: 现有的单标量Gas计量模型在多维资源需求和状态敏感性面前失效。研究结果强调了采用多维Gas定价机制和对状态增长进行显式定价的必要性，以提高交易的可预测性和执行效率。

Abstract: Gas metering on EVM-compatible blockchains assumes that execution conditions are stable: that the resource mix is constant enough to justify collapsing execution costs into a single scalar with fixed relative prices, and that state drift between submission and execution does not materially alter a transaction's outcome. We measure the extent to which this assumption fails.
  We present a trace-level measurement study of EVM workloads on Ethereum (L1) and Base (L2) throughout 2025, sampling 3,000 blocks per day per chain. We decompose each transaction into opcode-level execution gas, intrinsic gas, refunds, and persistent state deltas. To measure state sensitivity, we re-execute transactions from September 2025 on older states and record how gas usage and storage access patterns change.
  We find the resource mix to be far from stable: on Base, storage reads and compute account for 29.2% and 24.3% of execution gas, while Ethereum devotes 34.9% to storage writes. Ethereum's gas limit doubling during 2025 shifted its own profile toward compute-heavier, Base-like patterns. Base also exhibits a higher fraction of cold storage reads (49.7% versus 39.6% on Ethereum). Persistent state growth, a permanent cost priced as a transient one, reaches 456 GB on Base versus 38 GB on Ethereum.
  Execution outcomes are equally unstable: gas estimates vary across nearby historical states for 46.0% of transactions on Base, compared to 13.9% on Ethereum, with especially high sensitivity for MEV and DeFi activity. Storage access patterns also diverge across states, limiting the effectiveness of access lists and complicating parallel execution.
  Our work provides an empirical foundation for multi-dimensional gas metering and explicit pricing of state growth. They show that state-sensitive execution behavior complicates workload estimation, directly affecting transaction predictability and user experience.

</details>


### [164] [Online Dynamic Batching with Formal Guarantees for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19989)
*Dian Li,Zekun Wang,Yaoru Wang,Jiahong Yan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 为了解决 LLM 训练中因样本长度不可预知导致的 GPU 资源浪费，本文提出了 ODB 系统，通过在线动态成批技术大幅提升了异构微调的训练吞吐量，同时无需复杂的预处理缓存或底层架构修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 训练流程中，样本的实际处理代价（如长度）在预处理完成前无法精确感知。传统的离线批处理器因“盲目”成批导致填充过多、内存浪费及 GPU 利用率低下，而预计算长度缓存（length cache）又增加了额外成本。

Method: 提出了“在线动态成批”（ODB）系统，将批次形成过程移至数据预处理、增强、标记化及视觉标记扩展之后进行，从而获取准确的样本成本信息。同时形式化定义并解决了“分布式组对齐问题”（DGAP），确保了在分布式环境下的死锁自由和同步对齐。

Result: 实验表明，ODB 在 Qwen3-VL 等模型及多个数据集上，单节点训练吞吐量提升了 1.58-2.51 倍，多节点场景下提升高达 1.71-3.78 倍，特定生产场景下甚至达到 4.43 倍，且训练质量与传统方式持平，大幅超越离线预算预言机（GMT/BMT）的效率。

Conclusion: ODB 通过在训练流程中实现更准确的在线动态成批（Online Dynamic Batching），在无需预计算长度缓存或修改模型核心的情况下，显著提升了异构 LLM 训练的吞吐量，且能保持与标准做法相当的模型质量。

Abstract: Modern LLM training breaks a core assumption behind offline batch samplers: the true training cost of a sample is only observable after preprocessing, augmentation, templating, tokenization, and multimodal visual-token expansion. Unless one pays for a preprocessing- and augmentation-dependent length cache, batch construction is therefore blind to the quantity that determines padding, memory use, and GPU saturation. We introduce Online Dynamic Batching (ODB), a DataLoader-side drop-in system that moves batch formation to this point of accurate observability while preserving DDP step alignment. We formalize this synchronization requirement as the Distributed Group Alignment Problem and prove deadlock-free bounded termination with default join-mode identity coverage and opt-in non-join sample-quota closure. ODB requires no model, optimizer, or attention-kernel changes and is released as online-dynamic-batching with lightweight trainer adapters. Across public 2B/8B Qwen3-VL runs on UltraChat/LLaVA/ShareGPT4o, ODB improves literal emitted-sample throughput vs. fixed-batch Standard by 1.58-2.51x on single-node Full FT/LoRA and 1.71-3.78x on two-node Full FT, with Standard-comparable quality; production MM-Mix reaches 4.43x. Against GMT/BMT offline token-budget oracles, ODB is within 15% on UltraChat/LLaVA and faster on high-CV ShareGPT4o: 2.24-2.39x single-node Full FT/LoRA and 3.06-3.69x two-node Full FT. Together, ODB occupies the online/drop-in regime for high-heterogeneity LLM fine-tuning: large throughput gains at Standard-comparable quality, formal DGAP guarantees, and no length-cache precompute or kernel rewrites.

</details>


### [165] [ARGUS: Production-Scale Tracing and Performance Diagnosis for over 10,000-GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20374)
*Jiasheng Zhou,Longbin Zeng,Clavis Chen,Ruiming Lu,Qinwei Yang,Leyi Ye,Ray Ying,Key Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ARGUS 是一种专为超大规模 GPU 集群设计的低开销（<2%）、细粒度且支持常态化部署的 LLM 训练性能监测与诊断系统，通过分层观测与渐进式分析实现了高效的故障定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 LLM 训练需要持续且细粒度的可观测性来定位性能问题，但传统的粗粒度监控无法准确定位根因，而细粒度分析工具开销过大（5%-30%）且产生海量数据，无法满足生产环境常态化部署的需求。

Method: 1. 分层观测：将观测拆解为 CPU 调用栈、框架语义、GPU 内核执行三个维度； 2. 高效压缩：通过统一数据流和极高压缩比（约3,700x）处理大规模追踪数据； 3. 渐进式诊断：通过“迭代-阶段-内核”三个层级自动隔离性能瓶颈与异常。

Result: 在 10,000+ GPU 集群中实现小于 2% 的常态化开销；将数据量降低约 3,700 倍；在六个月的生产实证中解决了包括计算掉队、链路衰退、流水线气泡放大及 JIT 停顿等多种复杂的性能问题。

Conclusion: ARGUS 证明了在超大规模（10,000+ GPU）生产环境中实现极低开销（<2%）的持续、细粒度可观测性是可行的，能够有效支持大规模 LLM 训练的实时性能诊断与稳定性保障。

Abstract: Large-scale LLM training requires always-on, fine-grained observability for effective performance diagnosis at scale. Coarse resource monitors alone cannot localize root causes, and fine-grained profilers incur prohibitive (5%-30%) overheads and massive trace volumes, making always-on deployment impractical in large production clusters.
  We propose ARGUS, a low-overhead, fine-grained, always-on tracing and real-time analysis system for training workloads in 10,000+ GPU-scale production clusters. ARGUS decomposes observation along the training call hierarchy into CPU call stacks, framework semantics, and GPU kernel execution, with always-on collection under a combined overhead of less than 2%. It builds a unified data pipeline and compresses raw kernel events by approximately 3,700x from 10 MB to 2.7 KB per rank per step. Its progressive diagnosis framework automatically isolates anomalous windows, straggler ranks, and degraded kernels through iteration-time, phase-level, and kernel-level analysis. Deployed for over six months on a 10,000+ GPU production cluster, ARGUS has supported continuous fail-slow detection and performance optimization. Our case studies further demonstrate its effectiveness across representative anomalies, including compute stragglers, link degradation, pipeline-bubble amplification, FlashAttention JIT stalls, and compute stragglers masked by communication symptoms.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Deontic Policies for Runtime Governance of Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19464)
*Anupam Joshi,Tim Finin,Karuna Pande Joshi,Lalana Kagal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 智能体复杂的治理挑战，本文提出了 AgenticRei 框架，引入道义逻辑以处理义务、豁免和策略冲突，提供超越简单访问控制的全面企业级安全治理方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略引擎（如 XACML, Cedar）主要关注权限控制（允许/禁止），不足以应对 LLM 驱动的自主智能体带来的复杂治理需求，如义务履行、事后通知、条件豁免及策略冲突处理。

Method: 提出了 AgenticRei 框架，该框架基于 Rei 框架构建道义策略语言（使用 OWL 描述），并在 LLM 外部部署高性能逻辑引擎进行运行时评估。支持义务生命周期管理、元策略冲突解决、豁免条款及领域本体推理。

Result: 证明了道义策略能够有效表达当前生产级引擎无法涵盖的安全和隐私约束，且该方法可与行业标准框架（如 A2AS）自然集成。

Conclusion: AgenticRei 利用道义逻辑语言弥补了当前策略引擎在处理自主智能体治理方面的缺失，为企业级 AI 智能体提供了更全面、可扩展的安全与合规治理框架。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic AI systems driven by Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce a new class of security, privacy, and compliance challenges: an agent that can invoke tools, manipulate data, install software, and coordinate with peer agents across organizational boundaries must be constrained not just by authentication and access control, but by the full structure of enterprise governance. This includes specifying what agents are permitted and prohibited from doing, what they areobliged to do after certain actions (e.g., notify the CISO), under what conditions a standing obligation may be waived, and which rules take precedence when policies conflict. This governance problem exceeds what current policy engines provide. Systems such as XACML, Rego, and Cedar address only the permit/prohibit subset of this governance structure. They do not provide obligation lifecycle management, meta-policy conflict resolution, dispensations that waive obligations in specific circumstances, and ontological reasoning over domain class hierarchies commonly found in applications such as healthcare, cybersecurity, or data privacy. We propose AgenticRei, which realizes key governance requirements such as obligations, dispensations, policy conflict resolutions, and reasoning over policies, as well as the basic permit/prohibit constraints. We use a deontic policy language built on the Rei framework, expressed as OWL (Web Ontology Language) and evaluated at runtime by a high-performance logic engine entirely outside the LLM. The same pipeline governs both tool invocations by the agent and agent-to-agent messages. We show through examples that deontic policies capture governance constraints around security and privacy that mostly cannot be expressed in current production engines. Our approach composes naturally with industry-standard frameworks like A2AS.

</details>


### [167] [Measuring Curriculum Alignment across Topical Coverage, Competency, and Cognitive Depth: A Longitudinal Framework Applied to CS2013 and CS2023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19469)
*Sherzod Turaev,Mary John,Saja Aldabet,Mamoun Awad,Nazar Zaki,Khaled Shuaib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合语义检索与人类专家审核的量化流水线，用于追踪本科计算机课程与国际教学准则（CS2013/CS2023）之间的匹配程度及演变，揭示了课程在覆盖广度上的恒定性与在新准则深度要求上面临的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学本科课程受国际准则指导，但目前缺乏可靠、可复现的方法来衡量课程对这些动态变化的准则的覆盖程度及随时间演变的覆盖情况。

Method: 1. 构建“人机协作”（Human-in-the-loop）流水线：将课程与指导方针表示为结构化语料库。
2. 检索技术：测试了七种检索模型，确定互惠排名融合（RRF）集成模型表现最佳；对比发现小型句子模型优于大型长上下文模型。
3. 验证机制：辅以人类专家判断，经由第二评估者验证一致性（Cohen's kappa为0.64-0.69）。
4. 深度评估：将方法扩展至能力陈述（Competency）和认知深度（Cognitive Depth）的对比分析。

Result: 1. 该课程对CS2023的覆盖率为49.7%，对CS2013的覆盖率为50.9%，覆盖率在十年间保持基本稳定。
2. 课程在能力覆盖方面表现良好（~88%），但达到CS2023建议认知深度的比例（76%）显著低于CS2013（95%），反映了新准则期望值的提升。
3. 识别出了持续性的结构性缺口（如并行计算、系统基础等），这些领域在两个版本的准则下均未得到充分覆盖。

Conclusion: 研究开发了一种可复用的“人机协作”管道，用于准确评估本科计算机课程对国际准则（CS2013/CS2023）的覆盖率。结果表明，尽管教学大纲发生变化，该课程体系的核心覆盖率保持相对稳定，但CS2023更高的深度要求揭示了课程在认知深度方面存在的潜在缺口，为课程改革提供了可靠的实证依据。

Abstract: Undergraduate computer science is governed by international curricular guidelines revised about once a decade, yet programs lack a reliable, reproducible way to measure how completely they cover the current guidelines and how that coverage shifts when the guidelines are restructured. We address this with a human-in-the-loop pipeline that measures a program's coverage of an external body of knowledge, applied longitudinally to one accredited BSc in Computer Science against Computer Science Curricula 2013 (CS2013) and 2023 (CS2023). The pipeline represents the program and each guideline as structured corpora, generates candidate course-to-knowledge-unit matches by semantic retrieval, and confirms them through human judgment under an explicit coverage definition. Of seven benchmarked retrievers, a reciprocal-rank-fusion ensemble was strongest, and a reputed long-context model underperformed a small sentence model, so retriever choice must be measured. Both maps were validated by an independent second rater (Cohen's kappa 0.64 for CS2023, 0.69 for CS2013). The program covers 49.7% of CS2023 and 50.9% of CS2013 knowledge units, near-constant across a decade. Extending the same retrieve-then-confirm design to competency articulation and cognitive depth shows that the program articulates the competency for ~88% of covered units under each guideline, yet delivers it at the recommended depth for 76% of present units under CS2023 against 95% under CS2013, a gap reflecting the newer guideline's raised expectations, not the program. The longitudinal comparison separates persistent structural gaps (parallel and distributed computing, foundations of programming languages, systems fundamentals), uncovered against both guidelines and ABET, from differences that reflect the standard's evolution. The instrument is reusable and available from the authors on request.

</details>


### [168] [Diffusion Language Models: An Experimental Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19475)
*Thomas Bertolani,Davide Bucciarelli,Leonardo Zini,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对当前主流扩散语言模型（DLM）进行了全面的实证评估，分析了推理超参数对性能与效率的权衡影响，为理解DLM的实际部署能力提供了基准和洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散语言模型（DLM）研究缺乏统一的评估协议和对模型性能与计算成本权衡的深度分析，导致不同模型架构间的优势与适用性难以直接对比。

Method: 在八个代表性任务（涵盖推理、编码、翻译等）中，对八个主流扩散语言模型进行了系统性的实证对比；分析了推理时期的关键因素（去噪步数、上下文长度、块大小、并行解掩码策略）对性能的影响；并通过受控实验在统一训练条件下对小规模模型进行了对比分析。

Result: 揭示了不同DLM架构在不同任务和执行预算下的优劣势；明确了推理时期的设计决策（如去噪步数与计算耗时的平衡）对生成质量的影响，量化了计算效率与生成性能之间的 trade-off。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型（DLMs）在性能与计算效率之间存在显著的权衡，其表现高度依赖于推理阶段的参数设计（如去噪步数、并行策略等）。尽管DLMs具备并行生成的潜力，但在实际应用中仍面临性能与效率的复杂博弈，研究为DLMs的评估及落地应用提供了基准与实践指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized language modeling through autoregressive generation, enabling strong performance across a wide range of tasks. Recently, Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have emerged as an alternative paradigm that generates text through iterative denoising rather than next-token prediction, allowing parallel refinement of entire sequences. While numerous diffusion-based architectures have been proposed, differences in evaluation protocols, datasets, inference budgets, and generation hyperparameters make it difficult to compare their capabilities and understand the trade-offs they offer. In this work, we present a systematic experimental analysis of modern DLMs. Specifically, we evaluate eight state-of-the-art DLMs across eight benchmarks spanning reasoning, coding, translation, knowledge, and structured problem solving, while explicitly considering both generation quality and computational efficiency. Beyond downstream evaluation, we analyze the impact of key inference-time factors, including denoising steps, context length, block size, and parallel unmasking strategies, and complement large-scale experiments with controlled comparisons of smaller models trained under identical conditions. Our analysis highlights the strengths and limitations of diffusion-based language modeling across different tasks, architectures, and inference budgets. We show that the behavior of DLMs is strongly influenced by generation-time design choices, leading to distinct trade-offs between performance and computational efficiency. Overall, our study provides practical insights into the capabilities and deployment characteristics of contemporary DLMs.

</details>


### [169] [Hidden Anchors in Multi-Agent LLM Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19494)
*Apurba Pokharel,Ram Dantu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过引入“内部锚点”对多智能体LLM审议过程进行建模，揭示了智能体在互相影响的同时保持自我信念的动力学机制，成功解释了审议过程为何能突破初始置信度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体LLM审议在提升推理准确率方面效果显著，但缺乏对其核心机制（即模型为何以及如何通过交互收敛或偏离初始意见）的系统性理论建模。

Method: 借鉴社会学中的意见动力学模型（DeGroot, Friedkin-Johnsen），将其扩展为包含“内部信念锚点”的闭环动力学系统。通过实验分析多轮审议过程，重构智能体的隐藏锚点，并检验其对预测模型行为（泛化性）的效果。

Result: 1. 证实LLM审议确实受隐藏锚点的驱动；2. 成功从审议过程中重构出该锚点；3. 解释了为何审议能产生超出初始信念置信度的“涌现”现象（即跳出初始意见的凸包）；4. 发现模型家族在锚点影响程度上呈现谱系特征。

Conclusion: 多智能体大模型审议并非简单的意见趋同，而是受隐藏的“锚点”（内部信念）驱动的闭环动力学过程。只有当锚点偏离初始信念时，审议才能超越初始置信度限制，实现涌现性的推理能力提升。该模型为理解和预测LLM的交互行为提供了数学基础。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM deliberation, where agents exchange and revise answers over several rounds, is increasingly used to improve reasoning and accuracy, yet how and why it works is rarely modelled. Such deliberation mirrors how humans reach decisions. As social animals we are pulled both by the group, the herd effect that classical opinion-dynamics models such as DeGroot and Friedkin--Johnsen capture, and by our own internal belief, which they do not. We model multi-agent deliberation as a closed-loop dynamical system in which each agent carries a hidden internal belief, its anchor, that continually pulls its opinion regardless of its neighbours. We show this anchor can be recovered from the deliberation alone, and that it explains a behaviour classical consensus rules forbid: an agent's confidence in the correct answer can climb past where any agent started, escaping the space (convexhull) formed by the initial beliefs. Checking whether the recovered anchor also predicts held-out runs (generalizes) gives a simple test for when a model is truly driven bysuch an anchor. Across three open-weight model families this is a spectrum, not all-or-nothing. All anchors' influence are about equally strongly, but they differ in where the anchor sits, and only when it sits far from the initial opinions does deliberation escape the hull and need the full closed-loop model.

</details>


### [170] [DeXposure-Claw: An Agentic System for DeFi Risk Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19501)
*Aijie Shu,Bowei Chen,Wenbin Wu,Cathy Yi-Hsuan Chen,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对去中心化金融（DeFi）中复杂网络信用风险带来的监管挑战，本文提出了 DeXposure-Claw 系统。通过结合图时间序列预测与确定性逻辑过滤，该系统能够提供可靠、可解释的监管决策，并配套相应的评估工具 DeXposure-Bench 解决了大模型在监管场景中易产生误报的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化金融（DeFi）的快速发展导致了复杂的网络信用风险。现有的通用型大模型代理在处理此类快节奏的任务时表现不佳，表现为容易过度解读微弱证据、盲目建议高风险干预，且缺乏符合监管标准的准确性评估手段。

Method: 1. DeXposure-FM：利用图时间序列基础模型预测未来的暴露网络；2. 确定性监控与压力测试：将预测结果转化为类型化的警报、归因信号和场景证据；3. 置信度门控：通过数据健康度和置信度检查来约束升级决策；4. DeXposure-Bench：提出了一个包含六个维度的评估基准，用于衡量监管决策的准确性和误报率。

Result: 基于五年真实周度数据的实验验证了该系统。DeXposure-Claw 能够有效减少误报，生成具备逻辑解释且符合监管要求的审计工单，并在多维评估基准上表现优异。

Conclusion: DeXposure-Claw 是一种有效的去中心化金融监管方案，通过结合时间序列预测模型、确定性监控和置信度约束，成功解决了通用 LLM 在监管任务中容易产生错误警报的局限性，并为监管决策提供了可审计的依据。

Abstract: Decentralized finance exposes supervisors to fast-moving, networked credit risks. General-purpose LLM agents fit this setting poorly: they over-read weak evidence and recommend high-stakes interventions, while existing evaluations offer no regulator-aligned way to measure the resulting false alarms. We introduce DeXposure-Claw, a forecast-grounded agentic supervision system that routes LLM decisions through structured evidence: (1) DeXposure-FM, a graph time-series foundation model, forecasts future exposure networks; (2) deterministic monitors and stress scenarios then turn those forecasts into typed alerts, attribution signals, and scenario evidence; and (3) data-health and confidence gates constrain escalation before DeXposure-Claw emits auditable supervisory tickets with rationales. We further develop DeXposure-Bench, a six-axis evaluation harness, whose decision axis scores tickets against a regulator-aligned absolute-loss ground truth and an explicit false-intervention rate. Experiments on five years of weekly real data fully support our system. Code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/DeXposure-Claw.

</details>


### [171] [LLM Doesn't Know What It Doesn't Know: Detecting Epistemic Blind Spots via Cross-Model Attribution Divergence on Clinical Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19509)
*Akshat Dasula,Prasanna Desikan,Jaideep Srivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究发现LLM处理临床结构化数据时缺乏认知深度（置信度虚高），通过引入跨模型归因差异分析与外部校准器，可在无需微调的情况下提升模型对自身预测可靠性的评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLM）在结构化临床数据任务中应用广泛，但其是否具备评估自身认知局限性的能力尚不明确，尤其是其口头表达的置信度是否具有实际参考价值。

Method: 采用跨模型归因差异分析（Attribution Divergence Analysis），对比Qwen 2.5 7B（LLM）与XGBoost（树模型）在临床结构化预测任务中的行为。同时引入SHAP特征归因作为干预手段，并设计了一个无须访问模型内部参数的跨模型校准器。

Result: 1. LLM口头置信度不可信（固定在0.85-0.93，与准确率无关）；2. 存在反向难度效应（LLM在简单任务表现反而不如复杂任务）；3. Few-shot与SHAP归因配合可显著降低归因分歧得分（ADS）并大幅提高准确率；4. 跨模型校准器能将期望校准误差（ECE）从0.254降低至0.080。

Conclusion: LLM在处理结构化临床数据时存在“冷启动”问题，无法仅靠内省实现自我认知。通过利用跨模型归因差异（attribution divergence）构建校准器，可以有效提供患者层面的可靠性评估，从而提升模型在实际医疗场景中的不确定性感知能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to structured clinical data, yet whether they can recognize the limits of their own knowledge on such tasks remains unexplored. We study this question through the lens of cross-model attribution divergence with the goal of reducing epistemic uncertainty for structured tasks, comparing Qwen 2.5 7B and XGBoost on a prediction task via attribution divergence analysis. We report four findings. First, LLM verbalized confidence is epistemically vacuous, it outputs a near-constant (0.856-0.937) regardless of whether accuracy is 49% or 75.3%, tracking prompt format rather than prediction quality. Second, the LLM exhibits an inverse difficulty effect: accuracy drops to 64.8% when XGBoost is 99% correct, but matches XGBoost (73.8% vs. 73.1%) when it is moderately uncertain. Third, few-shot examples and SHAP-derived feature evidence are orthogonal, super-additive interventions: they reduce the Attribution Disagreement Score (ADS) from 1.54 to 0.38 and improve accuracy from 49% to 75.3% without training. Fourth, a cross-model calibrator that determined LLM reliability using attribution divergence signals reduces expected calibration error from 0.254 to 0.080, replacing uninformative verbalized confidence with patient-specific reliability estimates, without accessing model internals or requiring repeated inference. We frame these findings as a cold start problem for LLMs on structured data and outline a path toward genuine epistemic self-awareness.

</details>


### [172] [REVEAL++: Differentiable Phenotypic Grouping for Vision-Language Retinal Modeling of Alzheimer's Disease Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19522)
*Ethan Elio Meidinger,Seowung Leem,Zeyun Zhao,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种连续表型结构的对比学习框架，通过可微的加权机制而非硬性分组，实现了对阿尔茨海默病风险更精准的多模态预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言对齐框架（如REVEAL）在预测阿尔茨海默病（AD）时依赖于离散的表型分组（Hard group assignments）。这种硬性分组限制了监督的灵活性，并将分组过程与表征学习解耦，未能充分利用疾病风险的谱系特性。

Method: 提出了一种在对比学习中引入表型结构的连续公式化方法。该方法不再将样本分配到固定簇，而是通过视网膜图像和临床风险档案的模态内嵌入相似度，导出一个可微的加权函数，定义软多正样本关系（soft multi-positive relationships）。同时引入了软目标对比目标函数（soft-target contrastive objective），实现跨模态对齐和表型结构的端到端联合学习。

Result: 在英国生物样本库（UK Biobank）的AD发病预测任务中，该框架在表现上始终优于离散分组对比学习和标准的视觉-语言基线模型。

Conclusion: 将表型相似性建模为连续信号优于离散的分组方法，该框架为在大规模人群数据中进行多模态神经退行性疾病风险建模提供了一种稳健且有原则的方法。

Abstract: The retina offers a noninvasive window into neurodegenerative disease, capturing subtle structural patterns associated with a risk of future cognitive decline. Vision-language alignment frameworks such as REVEAL have shown that pairing retinal fundus images with structured clinical risk narratives improves early prediction of Alzheimer's disease (AD). A key design choice in these approaches is the use of phenotypic grouping, where individuals with similar risk profiles are treated as multi-positive pairs during contrastive learning. However, existing methods operationalize phenotypic similarity as a discrete construct, relying on hard group assignments that impose rigid supervision and decouple group formation from representation learning. We propose a continuous formulation of phenotypic structure within contrastive learning. Rather than assigning samples to fixed clusters, we model inter-subject similarity as a differentiable weighting function derived from intra-modality embedding similarities in both retinal images and risk profiles. These weights define soft multi-positive relationships through a continuous aggregation operator, enabling graded supervision that reflects the spectrum nature of disease risk. We further introduce a soft-target contrastive objective that jointly learns cross-modal alignment and phenotypic structure in an end-to-end manner. Evaluated on UK Biobank retinal imaging data for incident AD prediction, the proposed framework consistently outperforms discrete group-based contrastive learning and standard vision-language baselines. By treating phenotypic similarity as a learnable, continuous signal rather than a fixed grouping rule, our approach provides a principled and robust foundation for population-scale neurodegenerative risk modeling from multi-modal retinal and clinical data.

</details>


### [173] [Beyond Accuracy: Measuring Logical Compliance of Predictive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20208)
*Guillaume Olivier Delplanque,Pierre Genevès,Nabil Layaïda,Zephirin Faure*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对机器学习模型缺乏逻辑一致性评估的问题，论文提出了一种名为RVS的量化指标，能够独立于预测准确率，通过自动生成SQL查询的方式衡量模型对预置逻辑规则的遵循程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习评估多集中于预测性能（如准确率、误差），忽略了高风险领域（医疗、金融等）至关重要的逻辑一致性。缺乏一种通用的度量标准来评估模型对逻辑或领域约束的遵循程度。

Method: 提出了规则违例评分（Rule Violation Score, RVS），该指标分别处理硬规则（严格约束）和软规则（统计规律）；支持在任何数据集和任何基于关系词汇的预测模型上进行评估，并可通过针对Horn子句自动生成的SQL查询进行计算。

Result: 在知识图谱链接预测和关系回归任务中对规则驱动、嵌入驱动和神经符号模型进行了测试。结果显示，虽然不同模型可能具备相似的预测准确率，但它们的逻辑合规性存在显著差异。

Conclusion: RVS是一种评估机器学习模型逻辑一致性的有效度量标准，它能够揭示现有评估指标（如预测准确率）无法捕捉的模型行为差异。它不仅能评估模型，还能评估数据集的逻辑一致性及辅助规则定义。

Abstract: Machine learning models are predominantly evaluated through predictive performance metrics such as ranking quality, prediction error, or classification accuracy. While these metrics effectively quantify how closely predictions match the ground truth, they do not assess whether model outputs respect predefined logical or domain-specific constraints. In high-stakes applications, including healthcare, finance, and autonomous systems, logical consistency can be as critical as predictive accuracy, yet no standard metric captures this dimension. We introduce the Rule Violation Score (RVS), a complementary evaluation metric that quantifies the extent to which a predictive model respects a given set of logical rules, independently of predictive accuracy. RVS treats hard rules (strict constraints) and soft rules (statistical regularities) differently, can be evaluated on any dataset and on any predictive model expressed over a relational vocabulary, and can be computed using SQL queries that are automatically generated for Horn rules. Beyond evaluating models, RVS can also evaluate the logical consistency of training datasets and help identify poorly defined rules. We evaluate RVS on three benchmarks covering knowledge graph link prediction and relational regression, including rule-based, embedding-based, and neuro-symbolic predictive models. Our results demonstrate that two models achieving comparable predictive accuracy can exhibit substantially different levels of logical compliance, revealing differences in model behavior that standard metrics fail to capture.

</details>


### [174] [Emergent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19527)
*Martin Kolář*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用模型自我审查（良知步骤）和DPO优化实现LLM自我纠偏的方法，成功在模型中实现了“涌现对齐”，有效预防了模型在复杂任务中生成的非道德内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大语言模型（LLMs）是否具备识别自身偏见/非伦理输出的能力，以及是否可以通过自我修正机制主动实现对齐，从而解决模型在特定应用背景下（如代码破解）涌现出的非伦理行为。

Method: 1. 引入“良知步骤”（conscience step）：让模型在输出前对其产生的推理过程和结果进行自我审查。
2. 扩展训练损失：在Direct Preference Optimization (DPO)中增加对齐组件，将模型训练导向更符合伦理的方向。
3. 自评价机制：利用模型自身的冻结副本（frozen copy）作为评价者，无需依赖额外的高性能模型。

Result: 在与以往研究相同的“涌现错位”（Emergent Misalignment）场景中，通过单条高层级的内省式提问引导训练，成功实现了“涌现对齐”，显著提升了模型在不同应用环境（微调、对抗性提示、零样本学习）下的道德安全性。

Conclusion: 研究证明，通过引入“良知步骤”（conscience step）和基于DPO的在线对齐技术，模型能够实现“涌现对齐”（Emergent Alignment），即使在复杂的恶意攻击场景下也能保持合乎伦理的输出，无需外部评估者。

Abstract: Can Large Language Models (LLMs) discern when their own outputs are misaligned with human ethics? And can they self-correct? We endow an LLM with a conscience step that reviews its own reasoning and outputs, and we extend the training loss with an alignment component using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to steer the model away from non-ethical outputs. The result is an online technique to align models in a wide range of applications: training, fine-tuning, adversarial prompting, and zero-shot learning. It does not require a weaker or stronger judge, relying instead on a frozen copy of itself. In previous work, the Emergent Misalignment scenario showed a range of emergent unethical behaviors from fine-tuning the model to hack code. Instead, we empirically show how to achieve Emergent Alignment: a single high-level introspective question steers training toward an ethical model under the same code hacking scenario.

</details>


### [175] [ITNet: A Learnable Integral Transform That Subsumes Convolution, Attention, and Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19538)
*Ashim Dhor,Rasel Mondal,Pin Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ITNet 是一种统一的神经网络架构，通过“可学习积分变换”将卷积、循环神经网络和 Transformer 归一化为同一数学形式，并在多种任务中验证了其通用性和高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习模型（CNN、RNN、Transformer）在数学上被视为相互割裂，作者认为这是由于对信号处理方式的不完整理解导致的，旨在通过统一的数学视角重建一种能够通过数据自适应调整行为的通用计算范式。

Method: 提出 Integral Transform Network (ITNet)，核心是一个依赖于位置和特征的双变量可学习核函数（由 MLP 实现），并结合平铺核融合（tiled kernel fusion）、重要性采样蒙特卡洛积分以及学习型低秩分解技术来解决计算效率与扩展性问题。

Result: ITNet 在 ImageNet-1K、GLUE、ModelNet40、VQA v2 和 NLVR2 等多个基准测试中，表现优于或比肩专有架构。同时，证明了卷积、注意力机制及各类循环模型均可作为 ITNet 的特殊参数化形式，且 ITNet 是连续算子的通用近似器。

Conclusion: ITNet 通过单一的学习交互机制统一了卷积、循环神经网络和 Transformer 三大主流架构，证明了这些模型本质上都是对可学习积分变换的特殊近似。该研究为设计更具通用性和弹性的神经网络架构提供了理论框架与实践路径。

Abstract: Convolutional networks, recurrent networks, and transformers each encode different inductive biases -- locality, sequential memory, and content-dependent pairwise interaction -- and have remained mathematically distinct since their inception. We show that this fragmentation reflects not a fundamental diversity in how signals should be processed, but rather incomplete views of a single underlying mathematical object: a learnable integral transform. We introduce the Integral Transform Network (ITNet), a unified architecture built around a learnable kernel that depends jointly on positions and features. This kernel is implemented as a small neural network, specifically an MLP, that models pairwise interactions, enabling the model to adapt its behavior from data. We show that convolution, self-attention (including multi-head), and autoregressive recurrence (including LSTM, GRU, S4, and Mamba) arise as special cases under appropriate parameterizations, and that ITNet is a universal approximator of continuous operators. To make this practical, we develop tiled kernel fusion, importance-weighted Monte Carlo integration, and learned low-rank factorization, enabling efficient and scalable computation. A single ITNet architecture with a shared operator and lightweight modality-specific encoders matches or exceeds specialized baselines on ImageNet-1K , GLUE, ModelNet40, VQA\,v2 and NLVR2. The results demonstrate that a single learned interaction mechanism can recover the behavior of all three architectural families from data.

</details>


### [176] [Uncertainty Decomposition for Clarification Seeking in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19559)
*Gregory Matsnev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM代理在面对模糊任务时表现不佳的问题，提出了一种轻量级的提示分解方法，不仅能够有效识别不确定性并主动寻求澄清，且在多个基准测试及不同规模模型中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的偶然/认知不确定性框架不足以应对交互式LLM代理，缺乏对任务欠指定（underspecification）的处理能力。受限于生产部署中黑盒API、延迟和无标签的限制，需要一种轻量级的、可部署的提示词不确定性估计方案。

Method: 提出了一种简单的基于提示的分解方法（Prompt-based Decomposition），将动作置信度与请求不确定性（u）分离，无需微调或对数概率，适应黑盒模型使用场景。引入了两个带有澄清需求的基准测试（WebShop-Clarification 和 ALFWorld-Clarification）。

Result: 与ReAct+UE和UAM相比，该方法在ALFWorld-Clarification上的澄清F1值分别提升了73%和36%，并在绝大多数测试模型中显示出性能领先，表明其具有良好的跨模型泛化能力。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于提示的简单分解方法，能有效区分动作置信度和请求不确定性，使LLM代理在任务模糊时能够主动寻求澄清，且该方法在多个主流模型上表现出极强的泛化性和性能优势。

Abstract: Recent position papers argue that the classical aleatoric/epistemic uncertainty framework is insufficient for interactive large language model (LLM) agents and call for underspecification-aware, decomposed, and communicable uncertainty representations that can unlock new agent capabilities such as proactive clarification seeking and shared mental-model building. Practical deployment constraints -- black-box APIs, interactive latency budgets, and the absence of labeled trajectories -- rule out logprob-based, multi-sampling, and training-based methods, leaving prompt-based estimation as the most viable family for surfacing such signals at deployment time. We answer this call with a simple prompt-based decomposition that separates action confidence from request uncertainty (u), enabling the agent to ask for clarification when the task specification is ambiguous. To evaluate it, we introduce two clarification-augmented benchmarks (WebShop-Clarification and ALFWorld-Clarification) in which 50% of tasks are deliberately underspecified, and systematically compare the proposed decomposition against ReAct+UE and Uncertainty-Aware Memory (UAM) across five LLM backbones (GPT-5.1, DeepSeek-v3.2-exp, GLM-4.7, Qwen3.5-35B, GPT-OSS-120B) on these variants together with the standard WebShop, ALFWorld, and REAL benchmarks for fault detection. Averaged across the five backbones, the proposed decomposition improves clarification F1 on ALFWorld-Clarification by 73% over ReAct+UE and by 36% over UAM, and leads clarification F1 on every backbone on WebShop-Clarification and on four of five backbones on ALFWorld-Clarification, indicating that the gains generalize beyond a single LLM.

</details>


### [177] [Analyzing the Narration Gap in LLM-Solver Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19588)
*Zunchen Huang,Songgaojun Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM-Solver混合推理系统中的“叙述”安全风险，证明了即便求解器本身是可靠的，攻击者仍能通过提示注入操纵最终呈现给用户的答案，系统在端到端层面缺乏鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的LLM推理管线日益依赖SAT/SMT求解器以提供逻辑保证，但现有的研究多集中于“形式化”与“决策”环节，忽略了将求解器输出转化为用户语言的“叙述”环节，导致原本可靠的逻辑保证可能在这一环节失效。

Method: 1. 将LLM-Solver循环建模为验证决策过程（Verified Decision Procedure）；2. 在提示注入攻击下对五个开源模型进行实证评估；3. 引入“证书门控（Certificate Gating）”机制以确保求解器判定的可靠性；4. 设计并测试“加固提示词（Hardened prompt）”作为缓解方案，并针对自适应攻击进行分析。

Result: 发现“证书门控”能确保求解器判定的可靠性，但攻击者仍可通过变换措辞和渠道反转已验证的结论；加固提示词能显著减少但无法彻底消除提示注入的影响，且在面对自适应攻击时依然脆弱。

Conclusion: 尽管在大语言模型（LLM）与求解器（Solver）的混合流水线中使用了形式化工具，但在“叙述（Narration）”环节依然存在安全漏洞。鲁棒性无法延伸至用户最终读取的答案，即便使用加固提示词也难以完全消除提示注入攻击带来的风险。

Abstract: Formal tools such as SAT and SMT solvers are increasingly embedded in language model reasoning pipelines when a safety or security critical question can be formulated in logic. Unlike chain of thought whose steps are sampled from the model distribution without formal guarantee, a solver produces a sound and independently verifiable answer. However, the soundness guarantee can be lost in the interaction between the solver and the model. The hybrid pipeline has three components: formalizing the question, deciding it, and narrating the result. Prior work has studied the formalization and decision, but not narration, which is the step that turns a formal tool's output into the user answer. To fill the narration gap, we first model the LLM-solver loop as a verified decision procedure. We further evaluate five open-sourced models under prompt injection, and we find certificate gating makes the solver verdict sound, while an adversary can invert a verified conclusion across phrasings and channels. We study the mitigation through hardened prompt that reduces injection significantly but cannot eliminate it and still suffers under adaptive attack. Combining the formal analysis and empirical studies, we show in the LLM-solver loop, robustness does not reach to the answer that the user finally reads.

</details>


### [178] [Configurable Clinical Information Extraction with Agentic RAG: What Works, What Breaks, and Why](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19602)
*Osman Alperen Çinar-Koraş,Marie Bauer,Sameh Khattab,Merlin Engelke,Moon Kim,Stephan Settelmeier,Shigeyasu Sugawara,Fabian Freisleben,Felix Nensa,Jens Kleesiek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对临床文档元数据缺失和复杂推理难题，本文提出并部署了 ACIE 系统，该系统通过代理式 RAG 构建完整的患者语境，并实现了 96.5% 的医生认可度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 AI 系统缺乏临床所需的元数据，导致标准 RAG 在处理复杂的患者病历时无法有效处理时间推理、文档间依赖关系和缺失元数据等问题。

Method: 部署了 ACIE（Agentic Clinical Information Extraction）系统，这是一种本地部署的代理式 RAG 流水线，能够跨越异构文档进行推理，并为所有答案提供来源证据（grounding）以供医生验证。

Result: 在涉及 7,326 次判断的评估中，临床医生对 ACIE 的提取结果接受率达到 96.5%，各类数据的接受率在 80% 至 99% 之间。

Conclusion: ACIE 成功解决了临床环境中异构数据处理和元数据缺失的问题，在真实临床任务中展现了高准确度和可信度，证明了代理式 RAG（Agentic RAG）在处理复杂患者上下文方面的有效性。

Abstract: Patient contexts span hundreds of heterogeneous documents and thousands of structured data points, yet the document-level metadata that AI systems need for retrieval and triage is absent or incomplete. Standard retrieval-augmented generation fails on this data, mishandling temporal reasoning, cross-document dependencies, and missing metadata. We deploy ACIE (Agentic Clinical Information Extraction) at University Medicine Essen: an on-premise agentic RAG pipeline that reasons over complete patient contexts and grounds every answer in source passages for clinician verification. We quantify the metadata gap, trace the architectural decisions it shaped, and evaluate extraction alongside an independent retrospective lymphoma registry study, in which nuclear-medicine physicians verify every extracted value against its cited sources. Across 7,326 judgments, clinicians accepted 96.5\% of extractions, with per-type acceptance ranging from 80\% to 99\%.

</details>


### [179] [Denoising Implicit Feedback for Cold-start Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19658)
*Gaode Chen,Shicheng Wang,Shikun Li,Rui Huang,Xinghua Zhang,Yunze Luo,Shipeng Li,Shiming Ge,Ruina Sun,Yinjie Jiang,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对冷启动场景下隐式反馈噪声严重的挑战，提出了DIF方法。该方法通过引入内容相似的“暖项”推断伪标签并建模不确定性，实现了对冷门物品噪声反馈的自适应校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式反馈中普遍存在噪声（如点击诱饵、位置偏差），且冷启动项比暖启动项更容易受到这些噪声干扰。现有启发式去噪方法在应对冷启动场景时效果不佳，缺乏专门针对冷启动项隐式反馈去噪的研究。

Method: 提出了一种名为DIF的模型无关去噪方法。其核心逻辑包括：1. 利用内容相似的暖启动（warm）物品推断冷启动物品的伪标签；2. 基于内容相似度建模伪标签的置信度并进行聚合；3. 通过引入相对熵和项冷启动状态评估噪声标签的不确定性，从而在样本层面自适应地修正噪声标签。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上进行了充分实验，验证了DIF方法的优越性。此外，该模型已在快手短视频APP上成功部署，在冷启动场景下显著改善了多项商业指标。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的DIF方法在冷启动推荐场景下的去噪效果显著，不仅在理论上合理，且在十亿级用户规模的短视频平台（快手）中得到了实际应用验证，有效提升了商业指标。

Abstract: Implicit feedback is widely used in recommender systems due to its accessibility and generality, yet it usually presents noisy samples (e.g., clickbait, position bias). Meanwhile, recommenders inevitably face the item cold-start problem due to the continuous influx of new items. We identify that cold items are more prone to noisy samples due to the aforementioned factors, and researchers often overlook the significance of denoising implicit feedback for cold items. Previous denoising studies usually identify noisy samples based on heuristic patterns, such as higher loss values, and mitigate noise through sample selection or re-weighting. However, these methods have limited adaptability and are ineffective in cold-start scenarios. To achieve denoising implicit feedback for cold-start recommendation, we propose a model-agnostic denoising method called DIF. First, user preferences for content remain stable, which allows us to infer pseudo-labels indicating whether a user is interested in a cold item through content-similar warm items. Furthermore, to improve pseudo-label accuracy, we model the confidence of pseudo-labels based on the content similarity between the cold item and warm items, and then aggregate multiple pseudo-labels for each sample. Finally, we explicitly estimate the uncertainty of the noisy sample label by considering its relative entropy and the cold-start status of the item, which adaptively guides the role of pseudo-labels to correct the noisy labels at the sample level. DIF's superiority is supported by both theoretical justification and extensive experiments on real-world datasets. The method has been deployed on a billion-user scale short video application Kuaishou and has significantly improved various commercial metrics within cold-start scenarios.

</details>


### [180] [Which Pairs to Compare for LLM Post-Training?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19607)
*Jiangze Han,Vineet Goyal,Will Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在大规模响应池中如何挑选最有价值的样本进行人类偏好标注，通过将该问题建模为采样设计问题，证明了采样策略对 DPO 后训练性能的影响，并提出了一种优于现有启发式方法的采样优化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前偏好对齐范式中，人力标注成本远高于模型推理成本。现有方法通常通过对少量响应进行标注来构建数据集，这可能并非最优。研究旨在解决如何以有限的标注预算，从大规模候选中筛选出最具信息量的对比对，以提升对齐效率。

Method: 将偏好对比策划表述为“采样设计问题”，通过分析 DPO 训练目标下标签选择对参数估计误差与策略次优性的影响，推导出一个信息矩阵，并据此设计出旨在最大化信息量的比较对采样策略。

Result: 理论分析提供了 DPO 策略次优性的上界与下界；实验表明，基于所提优化框架的采样策略在合成环境及大模型后训练基准测试中，相比常用启发式采样具有更高的样本效率。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了对“偏好对比”进行最优采样设计的理论框架，证明了对比选择通过信息矩阵影响模型性能。通过该框架导出的优化方案在效率上显著优于现有的启发式采样方法，为大规模偏好数据集的构建提供了理论依据与实践指导。

Abstract: Preference-based post-training has become a central paradigm for aligning language models. A common data-collection strategy is to generate a small set of completions for each prompt and label the resulting comparison pairs. However, human preference labels are often much more expensive than generating additional completions, suggesting a different use of the same labeling budget: generate a larger pool of completions, but label only the most informative comparison pairs. This paper studies which pairs should be compared in preference-based post-training. We formulate comparison curation as a sampling-design problem and evaluate designs by the quality of the final policy under the preference-based post-training objective. We instantiate this framework for Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), analyzing how the choice of labeled pairs propagates through DPO training to downstream policy performance. Our main results provide matching upper and lower bounds on the post-training optimality gap of the DPO-trained policy. The bounds show that comparison selection affects downstream performance through a single design-dependent information matrix, which links label allocation to parameter estimation error and policy suboptimality. This yields an explicit optimization criterion for budgeted comparison curation and motivates practical sampling designs for selecting informative pairs from large generated completion pools. Experiments on synthetic settings and language-model post-training benchmarks show that the proposed designs consistently improve sample efficiency over common comparison-selection heuristics.

</details>


### [181] [Toten: Knowledge-Based Ontological Tokenization Of Physical Quantities And Technical Notation In Brazilian Portuguese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19626)
*Antonio de Sousa Leitão Filho; Allan Kardec Duailibe Barros Filho; Fabrício Saul Lima; Selby Mykael Lima dos Santos; Rejani Bandeira Vieira Sousa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TOTEN 是一种针对工程领域文本的本体分词框架，通过引入外部知识库替代单纯的统计分词，实现了物理单位和数值的高度原子性与结构化保留，有效解决了传统分词导致的语义破碎问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 BPE 等统计分词方法虽然在高频词压缩方面表现出色，但对工程物理量、数值、单位和符号表达式等具有特定语义结构的实体“盲目”，导致这些关键信息在分词后被切分为无意义的碎片，严重影响了后续对专业文本的理解和语义提取。

Method: 提出了一种名为 TOTEN 的知识本体分词框架，其核心是由 <本体 O、分类函数 classify、实例化器 inst_tau> 组成的架构。该方法通过结合三个外部预言机（Pint 量纲库、Unicode 字符数据库和 RSLP 葡萄牙语形态学解析器），将原始文本确定性地映射为具有工程语义的结构化类型化区域。

Result: TOTEN 在 EngQuant 基准测试及四个外部语料库上表现优异。在单位原子性保持上达到 100% 的准确率；在数值重构任务中，TOTEN 的准确率为 0.775-0.904，显著优于最佳基线模型 Quantulum3（0.627-0.703）；且各项改进均经 McNemar 检验具有统计学显著性。

Conclusion: TOTEN 通过将工程领域的本体知识融入分词过程，成功解决了传统统计分词（如 BPE）在处理结构化技术实体时语义碎片化的问题。研究证明，基于本体论的显式分类方法在保持实体原子性和数值重构精度方面显著优于现有的统计分词和专用工具，为工程文本的知识提取提供了更可靠的底层表示。

Abstract: Byte-Pair Encoding tokenization is statistically efficient for vocabulary compression, but semantically blind to structured technical entities, fragmenting physical quantities, numbers, units, and symbolic expressions into lexically arbitrary subwords. We present TOTEN, a knowledge-based ontological tokenization framework that replaces statistical derivation with declarative classification grounded in a formal ontology of engineering entities (OEE). We formalize TOTEN as the triple <O, classify, {inst_tau}>: the ontology gathers types, structural principles, composition relations, and preservable invariants; the classification function maps raw text into typed regions; and the instantiator family yields a self-descriptive structured representation. Robustness derives from deterministic coupling with three external oracles: Pint (dimensional), Unicode Character Database (typographic), and RSLP (Portuguese morphology). Intrinsic evaluation covers four properties verifiable by construction -- ontological atomicity, dimensional equivalence, typographic robustness, and numerical reconstruction -- over an internal, physically validated benchmark (EngQuant, N=800) and four Brazilian Portuguese external corpora (N=1771 eligible cases). We also report detection recall, distinguishing coverage from conditional atomicity. Against eight state-of-the-art baselines, TOTEN achieves unit ontological atomicity in all contrasts and numerical reconstruction of 0.775-0.904 on external corpora, vs. 0.627-0.703 for the best baseline (Quantulum3); on EngQuant, 0.780 vs. 0.340. Differences are statistically significant (McNemar with Holm correction). Spearman correlation between internal and external rankings confirms concurrent validity of the control benchmark. Dimensional equivalence shows statistical parity with Pint, the oracle from which the system inherits dimensional authority.

</details>


### [182] [Multi-Agent Transactive Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19911)
*To Eun Kim,Xuhong He,Dishank Jain,Ambuj Agrawal,Negar Arabzadeh,Fernando Diaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM智能体轨迹易丢失且难以重用的问题，本论文提出了MATM框架，通过建立共享轨迹记忆池，使智能体群体能够自动协同并重用程序性经验，从而优化任务执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在去中心化部署中，其执行完成的轨迹（包含丰富的程序性知识）常被丢弃或孤立，导致重复性的探索和资源浪费，亟需一种跨智能体群体的知识共享与重用基础设施。

Method: 提出了“多智能体交互记忆”（Multi-Agent Transactive Memory, MATM）框架，通过构建共享知识库，实现智能体生成轨迹（trajectories）的存储与检索，使消费者智能体能够利用生产者智能体生成的程序性知识来提升任务执行能力。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebArena等交互式环境中，MATM框架通过检索并利用群体轨迹，显著提升了下游任务的成功率，并减少了任务执行所需的交互步骤。

Conclusion: MATM 证明了一种无需联合训练或显式协调的机制，能有效地实现智能体群体间的经验共享，是开放式智能体生态系统中进行知识重用与协同进化的设计范式。

Abstract: The decentralized deployment of LLM agents with diverse capabilities across diverse tasks motivates infrastructure for knowledge sharing across heterogeneous agent populations. Just as search engines index human-generated artifacts to support human problem solving, retrieval systems can organize agent-generated artifacts for reuse across agent populations. We extend retrieval-augmented generation - which demonstrates the value of human-authored artifacts to individual agents - to retrieval of agent-generated artifacts supporting a population of agents. In particular, agent trajectories encode reusable procedural knowledge, yet these artifacts are typically discarded after a single use or retained only by the producing agent, forcing newly instantiated agents to repeatedly rediscover existing solutions. We propose Multi-Agent Transactive Memory (MATM), a framework for population-level storage and retrieval of agent-generated trajectories, where producer agents contribute trajectories to a shared repository and consumer agents retrieve them to improve task execution. We focus on interactive environments (ALFWorld and WebArena), where trajectories are long and encode especially rich procedural structure. Our experiments demonstrate that retrieving trajectories from MATM improves downstream task performance and reduces interaction steps without coordination or joint training. These results position MATM as a design pattern for population-level experience sharing in open agent ecosystems.

</details>


### [183] [AI4SE and SE4AI Exploration: A Decade Looking Back and Forward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19630)
*H. Sinan Bank,Daniel R. Herber,Thomas Bradley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文回顾了AI在系统工程（SE）领域的发展历程，通过结合人类专家与AI模型对逾2600篇文献进行评估，识别了领域内的关键研究缺口，并推出了AI4SE/SE4AI Explorer工具以辅助从业者应对AI采用与劳动力转型的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
