TL;DR: 本文提出了一套专为游戏AI设计的强化学习训练框架，通过分析实践案例与技术瓶颈，探讨了如何克服现有局限，以推动机器学习在游戏行业中实现更广泛、更具真实感的角色交互应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手写代码系统难以实现复杂的游戏AI行为，导致玩家体验受损；而利用机器学习（特别是强化学习）有望创造更具真实感和互动性的游戏角色，但目前该技术在游戏工业界的广泛部署仍面临诸多限制。

Method: 1. 提出了一个专为游戏AI和游戏开发需求设计的强化学习模型训练框架；2. 通过具体游戏案例展示了强化学习增强型AI的应用；3. 剖析了部署面向玩家的机器学习智能体时的实践问题；4. 识别并梳理了该领域当前面临的关键技术瓶颈及未来研究方向。

Result: 构建了一套适用于游戏开发流程的强化学习训练框架，明确了当前将机器学习应用于游戏AI的落地难点，并为加速该领域的研究进度提供了理论与实践参考。

Conclusion: 强化学习在游戏AI中具有巨大潜力，但需解决当前研究的局限性。本文提出的框架及所指出的瓶颈和研究方向，旨在促进机器学习技术在游戏工业界的实际部署与应用。

Abstract: Immersion in video games depends not only on graphics, audio, and game mechanics, but also on the quality of in-game characters. Producing believable characters, or game AI, remains a significant challenge as behavioral complexity is hard to capture with hand-coded systems. Game AI is a source of immersion and engagement; however, the limitations stemming from the challenges of creating game AI often lead to frustration and the breaking of the illusion of realism within the game. The introduction of machine learning models opens the door to creating more believable, authentic, and relatable characters in games. The promise is that they either learn from interacting with the game, or from player data, to develop true human-like behavior. In this paper, we envision more applications of reinforcement learning for game AI in the future. For this to materialize, current research limitations are prohibitive to broad deployment across game genres. Therefore, we propose a framework for training reinforcement learning models with a set of requirements in mind that are suited towards game AI and game development. We present examples of games with reinforcement learning-augmented game AI and describe the practicalities of deploying player-facing machine learning agents in modern games. Furthermore, we identify bottlenecks and hard problems in these areas, which we believe offer promising research directions to accelerate the adoption of machine learning in game AI for the video game industry.

</details>


### [220] [QMFOL: Benchmarking Large Language Model Reasoning via Quantifiable Monadic First-Order Logic Test Case Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20227)
*Xinyi Zheng,Ling Shi,Tianlong Yu,Yongxin Zhao,Lorenz Goette,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 QMFOL 框架，用于生成逻辑复杂度可控的演绎推理任务，并构建了配套基准 QMFOLBench，揭示了当前主流模型在处理复杂逻辑推理时的性能局限与一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理基准测试难以精确控制逻辑复杂度，且在语义多样性与逻辑一致性之间难以平衡，无法满足对 LLM 推理能力进行细粒度评估的需求。

Method: 提出名为 QMFOL 的自动化框架，使用合取和析取模式构建一阶逻辑结构（可控深度、宽度、标签和干扰项），并通过 LLM 将其转化为自然语言，结合外部证明器进行往返验证以确保逻辑一致性。构建包含 2880 个实例的 QMFOLBench 基准进行评估。

Result: 在六个 LRM 和两个 LLM 上的测试结果表明：模型性能随逻辑复杂度增加而下降，计算开销增加；模型在 True 标签任务上表现优于 False 或 Unknown 任务，且对语义变化表现出敏感性。

Conclusion: QMFOL 框架通过实现逻辑复杂度的可量化与可控，为大规模语言模型的演绎推理能力评估提供了一种可靠、可扩展的方法，揭示了模型性能随逻辑复杂性增加而下降的趋势。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made significant progress in reasoning, particularly in deductive reasoning, which is crucial for high-stakes decision-making. As models improve, evaluation benchmarks should evolve to keep pace. However, existing benchmarks lack fine-grained control over logical complexity and struggle to balance semantic diversity with logical consistency.
  To address these issues, we propose QMFOL, an automated framework for generating monadic first-order logic reasoning tasks with quantifiable and controllable complexity. It constructs formal logical structures using conjunction and disjunction patterns, enabling precise control over reasoning depth, width, label types, and distractors. These structures are then translated into natural language via LLMs, with logical consistency ensured through round-trip verification using an external prover. Based on our framework, we build QMFOLBench, a benchmark comprising 2880 instances with 960 configurations across diverse logical and semantic dimensions. Evaluations on six large reasoning models (LRMs) and two LLMs show that performance degrades and computational overhead increases with rising logical complexity. Models perform better on True-labeled tasks than on False or Unknown ones, and exhibit sensitivity to semantic variation. Overall, QMFOL offers a scalable and reliable approach for constructing deductive reasoning benchmarks with controllable complexity, enabling more precise evaluation of reasoning capabilities in modern language models.

</details>


### [221] [Thermodynamic Measure of Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20231)
*Ishanu Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于递归自我模拟和热力学原理的普遍智能度量框架，证明了系统识别并实现“罕见有效未来”的能力是智能的关键，且这种能力与系统的内部模拟能力直接相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决如何从物理学和热力学角度对智能进行普遍度量的问题，并探讨智能系统为了实现“对罕见有效未来的放大”所必须具备的内在结构。

Method: 通过建立热力学框架，将智能定义为“对罕见但有效的未来的合法放大（lawful amplification of rare but valid futures）”。该方法基于系统内部对世界及其自身建模的递归自我模拟假设，并推导出智能度量与内部模拟保真度及策略有效性之间的数学关系。

Result: 研究证明了“罕见有效提升（rare-valid lift）”与内部模拟保真度之间的理论联系：高水平的提升要求系统能高保真地识别此类未来；在模拟保真度高且包含有效策略的情况下，系统可达到受驱动限制的最优智能水平。

Conclusion: 递归自我模拟既是智能的合理特征，也是实现高热力学智能的必要且近乎充分的条件。该框架为从被动物质、反馈控制器到大语言模型和人类等各种实体提供了一种通用的智能度量标准。

Abstract: Can intelligence be measured? We propose that intelligence can be defined as the lawful amplification of rare but valid futures: a system increases the probability of outcomes that would be unlikely under passive dynamics but remain admissible under the constraints of the domain. We start with the premise that an intelligent system must model the world and its own place within it. Because the system is part of the world it models, this leads naturally to recursive self-simulation: the system represents futures in which its own actions are part of the trajectory. Our central results give a necessity statement and a conditional near-sufficiency statement connecting this architecture to a precise thermodynamic measure of lawful amplification of rare-valid futures: high rare-valid lift is impossible unless the internal simulation identifies rare-valid futures with high fidelity; conversely, when rare-valid fidelity is high and the simulation contains an effective policy, the achievable lift approaches the actuation-limited optimum. Thus recursive self-simulation is not merely a plausible feature of intelligence but, under the stated assumptions, is necessary and nearly sufficient for high thermodynamic intelligence. The resulting framework makes intelligence measurable on a universal scale, from passive matter and feedback controllers, large language models, and humans as text generators to Maxwell-demon-like information engines.

</details>


### [222] [A Multi-Agent system for Multi-Objective constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20236)
*Federica Filippini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了MAMO多智能体框架，通过将惩罚权重的选择过程建模为学习任务，实现了强化学习在处理动态约束优化问题时的自主权重调整，克服了手动调节参数的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习在处理带约束的动态优化问题时，通常将约束转化为加权惩罚项，依赖人工设置权重。这种方法在动态环境中难以调整，难以在优化目标与约束违规之间取得长期平衡。

Method: 提出MAMO（Multi-Agent system for Multi-Objective constrained optimization）框架，采用多智能体强化学习机制。将“惩罚权重选择”建模为一个学习问题，使系统能够在运行过程中自主调整目标函数权重，平衡主任务优化与约束满足。

Result: 实验表明，MAMO能够有效解耦任务执行与目标权重的设计，实现了在非平稳环境下的自动参数调整，从而提升了系统处理约束优化问题的鲁棒性与自主性。

Conclusion: MAMO通过多智能体强化学习成功解耦了任务执行与目标设计，实现了在动态环境中对惩罚权重（reward weights）的自适应学习，为解决约束优化问题提供了一种更具自主性和鲁棒性的框架。

Abstract: Many decision-making problems in computing and networking systems can be naturally formulated as cost-minimization problems under performance constraints. In dynamic environments, reinforcement learning (RL) is often used to solve such problems at runtime by embedding both costs and constraint violations into a single scalar reward through weighted penalty terms, following a Lagrangian-inspired formulation. However, in this context the behavior of the learned policy critically depends on the choice of these weights, which are typically selected manually. This makes it difficult to identify an appropriate trade-off between optimizing the primary objective and effectively avoiding constraint violations, particularly in non-stationary environments where their relative importance may change. This paper presents MAMO (Multi-Agent system for Multi-Objective constrained optimization), an approach to tackle this balancing problem through multi-agent RL. MAMO decouples task execution from objective design by formulating the selection of reward weights as a learning problem, providing a !rst step towards more autonomous and robust RL-based solutions for constrained optimization problems in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [223] [Navigating Unreliable Parametric and Contextual Knowledge: Explicit Knowledge Conflict Resolution for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20245)
*Huang Peng,Jiuyang Tang,Weixin Zeng,Hao Xu,Xiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型中内部知识与外部信息、或多源外部信息间存在的冲突问题，本文提出MACR框架，利用基于语义熵的知识评估与多智能体推理机制，实现对冲突的精准分析与有效解决，优于单纯依赖单一来源的传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在使用外部知识时，往往简单地优先选择模型内部知识或外部上下文，忽视了两者可能同时存在错误或矛盾的情况，且缺乏主动处理知识冲突的机制。

Method: 提出了MACR框架：1. 使用改进的语义熵评估LLM内部知识的置信度，决定是利用内部知识还是检索外部知识；2. 构建多智能体推理系统，包含三个专门智能体（分别负责归纳规则、分析冲突、解决一致性），通过归纳推理解决上下文及内部信息间的冲突。

Result: 实验结果表明，MACR框架在多个基准测试中显著优于当前的SOTA方法，并能提供冲突解决过程的解释性。

Conclusion: MACR框架通过引入多智能体推理机制，有效解决了LLM在内部知识与外部知识以及外部知识之间存在的冲突问题，提供了一种比传统二元选择（仅依赖内部或外部）更鲁棒、更具解释性的知识整合方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across a wide range of language-based tasks by leveraging both extensive parametric knowledge and in-context learning ability, enabling them to incorporate external information provided in the input prompt. However, the integration of external knowledge can introduce conflicts, not only between the model's internal parametric knowledge and the external information, but also among multiple pieces of external contexts. Existing approaches typically assume that either the model or the provided context is reliable, overlooking the possibility that both sources may contain errors, and avoid conflicts by privileging one source over the other, rather than actively resolving inconsistencies. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework MACR for LLM knowledge conflict resolution that moves beyond the conventional binary choice paradigm and incorporates an explicit conflict-resolution mechanism based on a multi-agent reasoning approach. Specifically, we first propose an adaptive knowledge assessment and retrieval approach that employs a modified semantic entropy measure to quantify an LLM's confidence in its answer to a given query. Based on this confidence estimation, MACR either externalizes the model's internal knowledge as textual representations or retrieves relevant external knowledge when internal knowledge is insufficient, generating basic contexts for subsequent reasoning. Then we introduce an inductive multi-agent reasoning framework with three specialized agents that, respectively, induce explicit rules, analyze potential conflicts, and resolve inconsistencies across all available contexts. Empirical results demonstrate that MACR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across benchmarks, while also providing interpretable resolutions of explicit conflicts.

</details>


### [224] [Confidence-Aware Automated Assessment of Student-Drawn Scientific Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20264)
*Luyang Fang,Yingchuan Zhang,Jongchan Park,Zhaoji Wang,Ping Ma,Xiaoming Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于视觉Transformer和置信度感知的自动化绘图评分框架，通过将高置信度结果自动计分、低置信度结果交给人工，实现了科学评估的规模化与可靠性优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学教育中学生生成的绘图是评估建模能力的重要手段，但人工评分成本高、难以在大规模课堂中推广；亟需开发自动化评估方法以解决扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 采用基于视觉Transformer（ViT）的模型并通过参数高效微调（PEFT）进行优化，同时提出了一种“置信度感知”评分框架，利用测试时的预测分布提取响应级置信度，以实现自动评分与人工复核的选择性协同。

Result: 在六项NGSS（新一代科学标准）对标的初中评估任务中，该方法表现出良好的评分可靠性，并能有效根据置信度筛选出需人工审核的案例，实现了科学绘图评估的自动化与高准确性的平衡。

Conclusion: 研究证明，基于置信度的自动化评分框架（结合视觉Transformer与参数高效微调）能够有效提升科学绘图评估的信度，并在自动覆盖率与评估风险之间实现平衡，为教育评估提供了一种值得信赖的实际应用方案。

Abstract: Student-generated drawings are widely used in science education to assess learners' conceptual understanding in modeling-based tasks aligned with the Next Generation Science Standards (NGSS). However, scoring such drawings requires expert human judgment to interpret complex visual representations, making large-scale assessment costly to implement and sustain in classroom settings. In this work, we study automated scoring of student-generated scientific drawings using a vision-based model. We evaluate a Vision Transformer (ViT) with parameter-efficient adaptation and propose a confidence-aware scoring framework that derives response-level confidence from test-time predictive distributions. This confidence signal enables selective automation by scoring high-confidence responses automatically while deferring uncertain cases for human review. Experiments on six NGSS-aligned middle school assessment items show that the proposed approach improves scoring reliability while supporting a practical trade-off between automated coverage and scoring risk, highlighting the value of confidence-aware methods for trustworthy educational assessment.

</details>


### [225] [Lagrange: An Open-Vocabulary, Energy-Based Sparse Framework for Generalized End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20274)
*Shihao Ji,HongXi Li,Zihui Song,Mingyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有自动驾驶在感知效率、泛化性与动力学约束之间的矛盾，提出了 Lagrange 框架，利用掩码潜在场和拉格朗日能量场优化，实现了兼具开放词汇语义理解与高频运动学规划的自动驾驶方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶模型在计算效率与泛化能力之间存在权衡：稠密模型（如占用网络）计算冗余且推理能力弱；稀疏查询模型受限于闭集定义；视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型难以处理连续高频的车辆动力学控制。需寻求一种既能开放词汇感知，又能保证实时运动学可行性的方法。

Method: 提出 Lagrange 框架，核心是基于掩码潜在场 (Masked Latent Fields, MLF)；利用视觉语言模型 (VLM) 将类无关的目标建议编码为连续语义视觉标记；引入意图驱动的掩码交叉注意力模块进行时空过滤；通过优化拉格朗日能量场来生成符合动力学约束的轨迹。

Result: 在 nuScenes（标准）和 CODA（长尾）基准测试中表现优异，证明了该方法在处理开放世界场景下的鲁棒性及运动学可行性。

Conclusion: Lagrange 框架通过将决策建模为拉格朗日作用量最小化问题，成功结合了开放词汇的语义推理能力与车辆运动学的严格约束，为实现稳健、可解释且符合运动学的开放世界自动驾驶提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Scaling end-to-end autonomous driving to complex, open-world environments requires perceptual models that generalize to anomalous scenarios and planners that produce kinematically valid trajectories. Existing paradigms face a distinct dichotomy between representational efficiency and generalization capacity. Dense models (e.g., occupancy networks), while geometrically robust, incur critical computational bottlenecks and struggle with high-level semantic reasoning. Conversely, sparse, query-based planners are efficient but reliant on closed-set definitions, rendering them vulnerable to out-of-distribution (OOD) events. Although recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer open-vocabulary reasoning, their autoregressive, discrete token generation fundamentally conflicts with the continuous, high-frequency control requirements of vehicle dynamics. To address this, we propose Lagrange, an open-vocabulary, computationally sparse driving framework based on Masked Latent Fields (MLF). Rather than relying on dense volumetric reconstructions or closed-set query mechanisms, Lagrange exploits Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to encode class-agnostic object proposals into continuous semantic visual tokens. We introduce an intent-driven masked cross-attention module that temporally filters irrelevant entities, decoding the attended tokens into an implicit continuous energy field defined over spatial coordinates. By framing decision-making as a Lagrangian action minimization problem spanning this energy field, we enforce strict compliance with vehicle kinematics while executing collision avoidance. Extensive offline evaluations on both standard (nuScenes) and long-tail (CODA) benchmarks demonstrate that Lagrange establishes a promising framework for robust, interpretable, and kinematically feasible open-world autonomy.

</details>


### [226] [Leveraging systems' non-linearity to tackle the scarcity of data in the design of Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20323)
*Giancarlo Santamato,Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Massimiliano Solazzi,Antonio Frisoli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对深度迁移学习在智能故障诊断中依赖大量标注数据的局限，本文提出一种利用系统固有非线性产生图像并结合改进数据增强技术的诊断方法，成功实现了在极度数据稀缺环境下的有效故障识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能故障诊断系统（IFDS）虽然依赖深度迁移学习（DTL），但仍存在对大规模标签数据的沉重依赖，而在许多实际工程应用中，获取足够的故障样本极其困难。

Method: 1. 利用系统固有非线性，采用周期性多激励水平程序（periodic multi-excitation level procedure）处理振动数据；2. 将振动信号转化为便于预训练卷积神经网络（CNN）分析的图像；3. 提出了一种新的数据可视化方法及其配套的数据增强技术。

Result: 在轨道交通受电弓（railway pantograph）结构的实验验证中，证明了该方法在强数据稀缺条件下具有显著的诊断有效性。

Conclusion: 通过周期性多激励水平激励结合图像化处理，能够有效解决数据稀缺场景下的智能故障诊断问题，为实际工业结构（如轨道交通受电弓）的故障监测提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep Transfer Learning (DTL) allows for the efficient building of Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems (IFDS). On the other hand, DTL methods still heavily rely on large amounts of labelled data. Obtaining such an amount of data can be challenging when dealing with machines or structures faults. This document proposes a novel approach to the design of vibration-based IFDS using DTL in condition of strong data scarcity. A periodic multi-excitation level procedure leveraging intrinsic non-linearities of real-world systems is used to produce images that can be conveniently analysed by pre-trained Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) to diagnose faults. A new data visualization method and its augmentation technique are proposed in this paper to tackle the typical lack of data encountered during the design of IFDS. Experimental validation on a railway pantograph structure provides effective support for the proposed method.

</details>


### [227] [SoftSkill: Behavioral Compression for Contextual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20333)
*Xijia Tao,Yihua Teng,Xinyu Fu,Ziru Liu,Kecheng Chen,Yuzhi Zhao,Suiyun Zhang,Rui Liu,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftSkill，通过将复杂的自然语言任务指令压缩为冻结模型前的紧凑潜在变量，在大幅减少Token开销的同时，显著提升了模型在复杂任务中的执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自然语言Markdown技能部署方式存在冗长、需模型实时解释且计算开销大的问题，研究旨在探讨是否能用紧凑的、可训练的连续上下文对象替代这些文本化的人工指令。

Method: 提出“SoftSkill”方法：将自然语言技能转化为紧凑的连续上下文对象（soft prefix），通过冻结主模型并优化其轨迹预测，将技能压缩为长度为32的潜在行为先验（latent behavioral priors）以辅助推理。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-4B模型上进行实验，SoftSkill在SearchQA等数据集上显著优于无技能提示（最高提升42.1点），且在准确率上超越了传统基于文本的SkillOpt方法；通过将数以千计的Token压缩为极少数虚拟Token，提升了 inference 效率。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将任务技能视为冻结模型中的紧凑潜在控制（SoftSkill），而非在推理时重新解释的大量自然语言Markdown，是一种更高效的行为指导方式。该方法实现了更高的性能和更低的推理计算开销。

Abstract: Agent skills are commonly deployed as natural-language Markdown files that encode answer policies, evidence-use habits, and task procedures. These files are readable and portable, but they are consumed indirectly: for each task instance, a frozen language model must translate a long textual artifact into generation-time behavior. This paper asks whether a natural-language skill can instead initialize a compact continuous context object, refined by a trainable soft delta while the base model remains frozen. We propose SoftSkill, a frozen-backbone method that tunes such soft skills with next-token prediction and deploys them as latent behavioral priors at inference time. In our main single-round setting, a length-32 SoftSkill prefix on Qwen3.5-4B improves over no-skill prompting by 8.3 points on SearchQA, 42.1 points on LiveMath, and 1.3 points on DocVQA. Relative to SkillOpt, SoftSkill improves accuracy by 5.2 points on SearchQA and 12.5 points on LiveMath, while replacing hundreds to thousands of Markdown skill tokens with a few virtual tokens. We further study agentic execution as a harder boundary case, where sparse trajectory imitation provides useful signal but does not yet robustly compress long-horizon procedural behavior. More broadly, the results suggest that some task skills are better treated not as additional Markdown to be reinterpreted at inference time, but as compact latent controls over how a frozen model enters the task.

</details>


### [228] [Automating SKILL.md Generation for Computer-Using Agents via Interaction Trajectory Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20363)
*Yuexing Hao,Xiaomin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过三阶段管道从GUI轨迹中挖掘技能库，虽然成功实现了高可读性的技能提取，但实验表明目前的挖掘技术在实现策略的跨域迁移与性能提升上仍然受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究是否能从交互数据中挖掘出显式技能库，以改善电脑操控代理（computer-using agents）的下游任务策略，并提升代理的可解释性。

Method: 构建了一个三阶段管道：(1) GUI 轨迹分割；(2) 将片段聚类为候选技能；(3) 利用所得标注训练技能感知策略 (GRPO)。

Result: 挖掘出的集群在源基准上具有良好的可读性（5/8 的集群纯度 ≥ 0.95），但迁移性能欠佳，仅能微幅提升技能步骤准确率，在多项关键指标上表现不及简单的频率先验。

Conclusion: 目前的轨迹挖掘方法（边界检测、无序段表示和离线奖励模型）在实现可靠的跨域策略改进方面尚显不足。轨迹挖掘可以揭示可检查的技能结构，但其对下游策略的提升效果仍非常有限。

Abstract: Explicit skill libraries make computer-using agents easier to inspect, but it remains unclear whether such libraries can be mined from interaction data in a way that improves downstream policies. We study this question through a three-stage pipeline that segments GUI trajectories, clusters segments into candidate skills, and trains a skill-aware policy from the resulting annotations. The mined clusters are readable on the source benchmark: five of eight clusters have at least 0.95 purity against InteraSkill Workflows labels. However, readability does not imply transfer. GRPO improves IW skill-step accuracy only from 18.5\% to 20.5\%, leaves BrowseComp+ essentially unchanged, and underperforms trivial frequency priors on key source-domain metrics. We therefore present the method as a diagnostic study: trajectory mining can expose inspectable skill structure, but the current boundary detector, orderless segment representation, and offline reward model are insufficient for reliable cross-domain policy improvement.

</details>


### [229] [Rethinking Shrinkage Bias in LLM FP4 Pretraining: Geometric Origin, Systemic Impact, and UFP4 Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20381)
*Qian Zhao,Kunlong Chen,Changxin Tian,Zhonghui Jiang,Haitao Zhang,Chaofan Yu,Peijie Jiang,Mingliang Gong,Jia Liu,Ziqi Liu,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现非均匀FP4（E2M1）存在几何不对称导致的“收缩偏差”，提出使用均匀格式（E1M2/INT4）的UFP4训练配方，在大幅降低显存与计算成本的同时，显著提升了训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的FP4训练路径（如NVIDIA Blackwell/AMD MI350）多采用E2M1格式，但在实际训练中存在不稳定性，缺乏一种高效、稳定的FP4训练方案。

Method: 1. 理论分析：指出E2M1格式因几何不对称产生收缩偏差，且会通过Random Hadamard Transform（RHT）被放大；2. 提出UFP4：一种基于均匀数值格式（E1M2/INT4）的4-bit训练配方；3. 优化技术：在所有三个训练GEMM中应用RHT，并仅对梯度dY应用随机舍入（Stochastic Rounding）。

Result: 在Dense 1.5B、MoE 7.9B及MoE 124B的长期预训练任务中，UFP4较E2M1基线表现出更低的BF16相对损失降级，且通过了缩放定律分析（Scaling-law analysis）和消融实验验证。

Conclusion: 现有的非均匀FP4格式（如E2M1）存在严重的“收缩偏差”（Shrinkage Bias），导致大语言模型训练不稳定。使用均匀数值格式（如E1M2/INT4）能有效解决该问题。研究建议未来GPU硬件应将E1M2/INT4作为原生训练支持格式。

Abstract: FP4 training promises substantial reductions in memory and computation cost for LLM pretraining, yet current FP4 hardware paths and recipes, including NVIDIA Blackwell/Rubin-class systems and AMD MI350-series GPUs, remain centered on E2M1 data elements. In this study, we identify a fundamental limitation of that choice: non-uniform formats such as E2M1 inherently suffer from Shrinkage Bias, a systematic negative rounding error caused by the geometric asymmetry of their representable bins. We show that this bias accumulates multiplicatively across layers and is amplified by the Random Hadamard Transform (RHT), providing a unified explanation for the training instability observed in existing E2M1-based FP4 recipes. In contrast, uniform grids (E1M2/INT4) bypass this grid-geometry error and better convert the improved bucket utilization from RHT into higher quantization quality. Based on this finding, we propose UFP4, a uniform 4-bit training recipe that applies RHT to all three training GEMMs while restricting stochastic rounding to dY alone. On Dense 1.5B, MoE 7.9B, and MoE 124B long-run pretraining, UFP4 consistently achieves lower BF16-relative loss degradation than strong E2M1-based baselines, supported by scaling-law analysis and ablation studies. Our results suggest that future accelerators should support E1M2/INT4-style uniform 4-bit grids as first-class training primitives alongside E2M1.

</details>


### [230] [Interpretable Sperm Morphology Classification via Attention-Guided Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20438)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Thomas Ebner,Lars Johansson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究构建了一种融合CBAM注意力机制的EfficientNet-B0深度学习框架用于精子形态分类，在保证高精度的同时通过Grad-CAM++提供了可视化决策支持，解决了临床应用中黑箱模型难以信任的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 男性不育常与精子形态异常相关，尽管深度学习能实现自动分析，但现有模型往往缺乏可解释性，阻碍了其在临床环境中的推广与应用。

Method: 1. 基于预训练的EfficientNet-B0作为特征提取主干网络；
2. 引入卷积块注意力模块（CBAM）以增强对精子头部关键特征的关注；
3. 使用Grad-CAM++技术生成决策区域的可视化图，提升模型的可解释性。

Result: 在SMIDS和HuSHem公开数据集上，该模型取得了90.2%和93.9%的准确率（宏平均F1得分分别为0.913和0.948），性能优于SimpleCNN及标准的EfficientNet-B0模型。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的结合CBAM注意力机制的EfficientNet-B0模型在精子形态分类任务中表现优异，提供了高准确率与可解释性（Grad-CAM++可视化），具备临床应用的实际潜力，可有效辅助男性不育诊断。

Abstract: Male infertility is a major cause of couple infertility, often linked to abnormal sperm morphology. While deep learning models offer automated analysis, most lack interpretability, limiting their clinical adoption. This study proposes an attention-guided deep learning framework for sperm morphology classification. We combine a pretrained EfficientNet-B0 with a Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) to focus on key areas of the sperm head, improving both accuracy and interpretability. Evaluated on the SMIDS and HuSHem public datasets, our model achieves accuracies of 90.2% and 93.9% (macro F1 scores of 0.913 and 0.948), outperforming SimpleCNN and standard EfficientNet-B0. Furthermore, we use Grad-CAM++ visualizations to highlight features influencing the model's decisions. The results demonstrate that this accurate and transparent framework is a practical tool for automated sperm analysis in fertility clinics.

</details>


### [231] [Context-Aware Hierarchical Bayesian Modeling of IVF Laboratory Environmental Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20459)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Pia Saldeen,Lars Johansson,Thomas Ebner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究证明了利用实验室环境的高分辨率动态数据（而非简单的平均值）能大幅提升IVF妊娠率预测精度，并验证了跨中心环境特征的迁移有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的IVF妊娠率预测通常仅依赖患者层面的变量，忽视了高分辨率实验室环境数据中潜在的预测价值，导致模型精度受限。

Method: 1. 特征工程：将原始传感器数据转化为55个上下文感知的时间序列特征（如滚动热稳定性、温湿度协同性、峰值应力持续时间等）；2. 模型架构：采用分层贝叶斯Beta回归模型，通过部分池化（partial pooling）技术共享多中心环境效应，同时保留各研究中心基线特征。

Result: 在亚洲样本上，新特征将预测误差降至1.27%（此前为3-5%）；在北欧临床中心持留数据上，模型R2值达到0.86，且在35-39岁年龄组中实现了64%的误差降低。

Conclusion: 通过构建上下文感知的时间特征并利用分层贝叶斯模型，证实临床实验室微环境数据包含可迁移的、具有临床意义的预测信号，可显著提升IVF妊娠率预测精度。

Abstract: IVF pregnancy rates are routinely modeled using patient-level variables, while high-resolution laboratory environmental data remain underutilized. We show that this is a missed opportunity. Rather than relying on raw sensor averages, we engineer 55 context-aware temporal features, including rolling thermal stability, simultaneous temperature-humidity adherence, peak stress duration, and post-stress recovery speed, that capture the dynamics of incubator microenvironments. On 61 weeks of data from an Asian IVF clinic, these features reduce cross-validated prediction error to 1.27%, compared to 3-5% for raw averages. We then train a hierarchical Bayesian Beta regression model that shares environmental effects across an Asian and a Northern European clinic via partial pooling, while preserving site-specific baselines. On held-out data from the Northern European clinic, the model achieves R2 = 0.86 and a 64% error reduction for the 35-39 age group over a naive baseline, demonstrating that structured environmental monitoring contains clinically meaningful, transferable signal.

</details>


### [232] [What Do Safety-Aligned LLMs Learn From Mixed Compliance Demonstrations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20508)
*Sihui Dai,Mann Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了上下文演示引发模型越狱的深层机理：模型的合规行为并非简单模仿，而是受内容构成、顺序偏好及训练方法（偏好优化）的复杂控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知上下文演示（ICL）可诱发模型越狱，但模型如何解释不同类型的合规演示，以及演示构成如何具体驱动有害合规的机制尚不明确。

Method: 通过混合良性与恶意演示样本，测试关于上下文演示（ICL）如何推动有害合规的三项假设；分析四种不同模型在不同演示组合、顺序及训练阶段下的行为表现；考察拒绝机制与上下文学习的交互模式。

Result: 1. 良性和恶意演示不可互换，良性演示对有害合规的影响在不同模型中表现不一（可能抑制或加剧）；2. 偏好优化（Preference Optimization）是抑制良性演示引发有害合规的关键训练阶段；3. 演示顺序存在明显的近因效应；4. 不同模型在拒绝请求时处理上下文信号的机制存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 模型如何根据上下文演示产生合规行为受演示内容（良性/恶意混合）、演示顺序（近因效应）以及模型训练方法（偏好优化）的共同影响。理解这些机制对于构建更稳健的防御策略至关重要。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that in-context demonstrations can jailbreak language models, but it remains unclear how models interpret different types of compliance demonstrations. We study this by mixing benign compliance demonstrations (non-harmful request, helpful response) with harmful compliance demonstrations (harmful request, helpful response) and testing three hypotheses about how demonstration composition drives harmful compliance. Across four models, we find that benign and harmful demonstrations are not interchangeable: benign demonstrations can either reduce or increase harmful compliance depending on the model. We further show that preference optimization is the critical training stage that prevents benign demonstrations from increasing harmful compliance, that demonstration ordering exhibits strong recency bias, and that models differ in how refusal interacts with in-context learning: some adopt demonstrated formatting even when refusing, while others override all in-context signals upon refusal. Taken together, this work moves beyond showing that demonstration-based jailbreaking works to characterizing how it works: what models extract from compliance demonstrations depends on demonstration content, ordering, and training methodology.

</details>


### [233] [Multi-LCB: Extending LiveCodeBench to Multiple Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20517)
*Maria Ivanova,Pavel Zadorozhny,Rodion Levichev,Ivan Petrov,Adamenko Pavel,Ivan Lopatin,Alexey Kutalev,Dmitrii Babaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为解决代码生成基准仅关注 Python 的现状，本文提出了 Multi-LCB，这是一个支持 12 种编程语言的动态评测基准。研究发现当前 LLM 在多语言编码能力上存在明显的过拟合和性能短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流行的代码生成基准（如 LCB）主要局限于 Python，无法衡量模型在真实软件工程所需的多语言环境下的泛化能力。

Method: 通过将 LiveCodeBench (LCB) 中的 Python 题目自动化转换为其他 11 种编程语言，同时保留了 LCB 的抗数据污染机制和评测协议，形成了一个涵盖 12 种语言的持续更新基准。

Result: 对 24 个大语言模型的测评结果表明，模型普遍存在 Python 过拟合、语言特异性数据污染以及多语言性能显著差距的问题。

Conclusion: Multi-LCB 作为一个针对多编程语言的严格评测基准，有效地解决了现有基准过于依赖 Python 的局限，揭示了当前大语言模型在多语言编码能力上的严重不足。

Abstract: LiveCodeBench (LCB) has recently become a widely adopted benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) on code-generation tasks. By curating competitive programming problems, constantly adding fresh problems to the set, and filtering them by release dates, LCB provides contamination-aware evaluation and offers a holistic view of coding capability. However, LCB remains restricted to Python, leaving open the question of whether LLMs can generalize across the diverse programming languages required in real-world software engineering.
  We introduce Multi-LCB, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs across twelve programming languages, including Python. Multi-LCB transforms Python tasks from the LCB dataset into equivalent tasks in other languages while preserving LCB's contamination controls and evaluation protocol. Because it is fully compatible with the original LCB format, Multi-LCB will automatically track future LCB updates, enabling systematic assessment of cross-language code generation competence and requiring models to sustain performance well beyond Python.
  We evaluated 24 LLMs for instruction and reasoning on Multi-LCB, uncovering evidence of Python overfitting, language-specific contamination, and substantial disparities in multilingual performance. Our results establish Multi-LCB as a rigorous new benchmark for multi-programming-language code evaluation, directly addressing LCB's primary limitation and exposing critical gaps in current LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [234] [FlowEdit: Associative Memory for Lifelong Pronunciation Adaptation in Flow-Matching TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20518)
*Harshit Singh,Ayush Pratap Singh,Nityanand Mathur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对固定流程匹配TTS模型，FlowEdit通过外部联想记忆（Modern Hopfield Network）实现零重训练的实时专有名词发音校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有的流程匹配（Flow-matching）文本转语音（TTS）系统在部署后无法实时修正专有名词发音错误的问题，避免昂贵的模型重训练代价。

Method: 引入FlowEdit框架，将语音校正视为潜在条件的编辑过程而非权重更新。具体包括：1. 在文本嵌入空间优化令牌级扰动；2. 利用Modern Hopfield Network构建内容可寻址的长期记忆以存储纠正信息；3. 推理时通过带相似度门的软注意力机制进行模糊形态匹配检索。

Result: 在包含18个语系、312个专有名词的基准测试中，目标词音素错误率降低了92.7%，且保持了原始语音质量，单次纠正推理耗时仅约15秒。

Conclusion: FlowEdit提供了一种高效、即时的流程匹配TTS纠错方案，通过外部记忆进行自适应，无需重训练模型，且在保持原模型语音质量的同时，显著降低了专有名词的音素错误率。

Abstract: Flow-matching text-to-speech systems achieve remarkable zero-shot quality but remain static after deployment: pronunciation errors on out-of-vocabulary proper nouns persist unless the model is retrained. We introduce FlowEdit, a life-long adaptation framework for frozen flow-matching TTS that learns pronunciation corrections as latent conditioning edits rather than weight updates. When corrective feedback is provided, FlowEdit optimizes a token-level perturbation in the text embedding space, then stores the correction in a Modern Hopfield Network serving as content-addressable episodic memory. At inference, corrections are retrieved via soft attention with a similarity gate, enabling fuzzy morphological matching. On our curated benchmark of 312 multilingual proper nouns across 18 language families, FlowEdit reduces target-word Phoneme Error Rate by 92.7% relative to the zero-shot baseline while maintaining identical general-speech quality. Corrections complete in approximately 15 seconds on a single GPU.

</details>


### [235] [DeepSWIP: Quotient-WMC Counterfactuals for Neural Probabilistic Logic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20526)
*Saimun Habib,Vaishak Belle,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSWIP 是一种为 DeepProbLog 程序设计的单世界反事实推理框架，通过神经具象化技术，在实现因果推理精确性的同时，显著提升了推理效率并纠正了神经模型带来的估计偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经符号系统（如 DeepProbLog）主要侧重于关联推理，缺乏处理反事实推理所需的因果语义，且传统的双胞胎网络方法在计算上存在冗余。

Method: 引入 DeepSWIP 语义，通过神经网络具象化（Neural Materialization）将固定上下文的神经谓词简化为标准的 ProbLog 选择，利用单世界干预程序（SWIPs）进行加权模型计数（WMC），避免了传统双胞胎网络（Twin Networks）的冗余建模。

Result: 经验证，DeepSWIP 在 MPI3D 数据集上产生了与 DeepTwin 等价的结果，推理速度提升了 2.14 倍；此外，在 SUMO HOV 实验中，证实了使用 AIPW 估计器可以有效消除神经校准退化带来的系统性偏差。

Conclusion: DeepSWIP 提供了一种精确的单世界反事实推理框架，在保持逻辑一致性的同时，通过消除冗余提升了推理效率（2.14倍），并有效缓解了神经模型校准偏差对反事实因果估计的影响。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems such as DeepProbLog combine neural perception with probabilistic logic, but standard inference is associational. Counterfactual reasoning additionally requires a causal semantics for interventions and evidence. We introduce DeepSWIP, a single-world counterfactual semantics for DeepProbLog programs. Using neural materialization, we reduce fixed-context neural predicates to ordinary ProbLog choices, apply Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs), and compute counterfactuals by weighted model counting (WMC) over a single transformed program. Under finite grounding and unique-supported-model assumptions, DeepSWIP is exact relative to the learned materialized FCM. The standard quotient-WMC form of ProbLog conditionals identifies active neural probabilities and explains intervention cleaning, calibration sensitivity, and rare-evidence instability. Experiments on MPI3D confirm the transformation against a DeepTwin construction against 12,000 queries, as predicted and a 2.14$\times$ inference speedup from avoiding the Twin's endogenous duplication. A SUMO HOV experiment shows that neural calibration degradation biases plug-in estimates, while a correctly scoped randomized-policy AIPW estimator removes most first-order bias for population mean and ATE estimands. Code is at https://github.com/saibib/deep_SWIP.

</details>


### [236] [LedgerAgent: Structured State for Policy-Adherent Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20529)
*Md Nayem Uddin,Amir Saeidi,Eduardo Blanco,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LedgerAgent 是一种通过显式维护独立任务状态账本（Ledger）来优化工具调用代理的方法，它有效解决了传统 Agent 状态管理隐式化带来的失效问题，并实现了更严格的策略合规控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理通常将任务状态隐含在 Prompt 中，存在状态丢失、过时或判断不准确的问题，且难以有效执行依赖于状态的复杂领域策略。

Method: 引入 LedgerAgent 架构，将任务状态提取并保存于独立的“账本”（Ledger）中；在进行环境变更工具调用前，基于该账本对策略约束进行校验，并实时将更新后的状态同步至 Agent 的 Prompt 中。

Result: 在四个客户服务领域及多种模型（开放和闭源）测试中，LedgerAgent 在平均 pass@k 指标上显著优于传统方法，并在更严格的多轮一致性指标上展现出最大提升。

Conclusion: LedgerAgent 通过引入显式状态管理和事后策略校验，有效解决了传统对话代理在维护任务状态和遵循复杂策略时的失效问题，显著提升了工具调用质量和策略合规性。

Abstract: Policy-adherent tool-calling agents in customer-service domains must maintain task states across turns while calling tools and obeying domain policies. Task states consist of relevant facts, identifiers, constraints, and conditions observed through user interaction and tool calls. In standard agents, task states are not represented separately. Observations, tool returns, and policy instructions are placed in the prompt, leaving agents to reconstruct the relevant states from the prompt each time they decide what to do next. This design makes state management implicit, creating two common failure modes. An agent may retrieve the right facts but later ground its decision in stale, missing, or incorrect information; and a syntactically valid tool call may still violate a domain policy that depends on the current task state. We introduce \textsc{LedgerAgent}, an inference-time method for tool-calling agents that maintains observed task states in a separate ledger and renders the states into the prompt. The ledger is also used to check state-dependent policy constraints before environment-changing tool calls are executed, blocking policy violations. Across four customer-service domains and a mixed panel of open- and closed-weight models, \textsc{LedgerAgent} improves average pass\textasciicircum{}k over a standard prompt-based tool-calling approach, with the largest gains under stricter multi-trial consistency metrics.

</details>


### [237] [How Do Instructions Shape Speech? Cross-Attention Attribution for Style-Captioned Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20532)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Wasim Madha,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次通过交叉注意力归因技术解析了语音扩散模型中自然语言提示词如何控制语音生成，通过定量分析揭示了风格词在空间、时间和网络层级上的具体作用模式，填补了语音合成可解释性研究的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的风格标注文本转语音（Style-captioned TTS）系统虽能通过自然语言控制语音特征，但缺乏对“个别单词如何具体影响声学输出”的透明度与理解，这制约了模型可控性的改进及故障诊断。

Method: 提出了一种针对语音扩散模型的“交叉注意力归因（Cross-attention attribution）”分析方法，将DAAM（扩散模型注意力归因映射）框架适配至语音领域，并应用于CapSpeech-TTS系统。通过提取跨越25个层和24个ODE步骤的按词（per-token）注意力热图，对3,600组（风格描述-文本转录）组合进行了定量统计分析。

Result: 实验发现：(1) 风格词与内容/功能词相比具有更低的临时方差，佐证了其全局调节作用；(2) 风格注意力与基频（F0）及能量特征显著相关；(3) 风格表征在扩散过程的早期步骤和网络深层达到峰值；(4) 模型在第17层处注意力熵达到最小值，且对应风格重要性峰值，反映了网络在该阶段拥有最高的选择性。

Conclusion: 本研究首次揭示了自然语言在语音扩散模型中通过交叉注意力机制影响音频输出的机制。研究证实了风格提示词在不同层级和扩散步骤中的特定作用模式，为评估和优化表达性语音合成（TTS）系统的可控性及诊断模型失效模式提供了理论依据和分析框架。

Abstract: Style-captioned text-to-speech systems use natural language to control voice characteristics, but how individual words influence acoustic output remains unclear. Understanding this is critical for diagnosing failure modes and improving controllability in expressive TTS. We propose cross-attention attribution for speech diffusion models, adapting the DAAM framework to the speech domain for the first time, and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS. Our method extracts per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps. We analyze 3,600 (style caption, text transcript) combinations comprising 120 style captions conditioning the generation of 30 text transcripts each, revealing how caption tokens shape waveforms. Results show: (1) style tokens have lower temporal variance than content/function tokens, confirming global conditioning; (2) style attention correlates with F0 and energy; (3) style conditioning peaks in early steps and deep layers; (4) attention entropy reaches its minimum at layer 17, co-occurring with the style importance peak, indicating maximal network selectivity at the most style-critical stage. This is the first study of how natural language influences cross-attention in speech diffusion models

</details>


### [238] [Toward Calibrated Mixture-of-Experts Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20544)
*Gina Wong,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合专家（MoE）模型在分布偏移下的校准行为，提出了对抗性重加权策略以解决软路由模型校准不足的问题，有效提升了模型在偏移环境下的鲁棒性与校准度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然专家级校准能改善MoE模型的性能，但其在分布偏移下的行为及作用机制尚不明确，特别是在软路由与硬路由模型间的差异。

Method: 针对MoE模型提出了一种对抗性重加权（adversarial reweighting）机制，通过惩罚分布偏移下路由聚合后的校准误差来提升模型表现。

Result: 实验证明该方法在多种模型类别、预测任务和分布偏移下，均能显著改善“准确性-校准”权衡，特别是在处理困难数据子集时表现突出。

Conclusion: 专家校准在硬路由MoE模型中足以确保整体校准，但在软路由模型中不足。通过对抗性重加权方法，可以有效改善分布偏移下的准确性与校准之间的权衡。

Abstract: Calibration aligns a model's predictive uncertainty with the frequencies of its empirical outcomes and is important for understanding and trusting reported probabilities. Recent work shows that enforcing calibration at the level of individual predictors can improve ensemble accuracy and calibration, with mixture-of-experts (MoE) models showing strong empirical improvements in particular; however, the conditions under which calibration helps MoE are not well understood. In this work, we study how MoE models behave under distribution shift, focusing on how routing mechanisms interact with expert-level calibration. We show that expert calibration is sufficient to ensure calibration of the overall model under a broad class of distribution shifts in hard-routed models, but is insufficient for calibrating soft-routed models. To address this, we propose an adversarial reweighting that penalizes calibration errors of the routed aggregate under distribution shift, and we demonstrate that it improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff both on average and on difficult subsets of the data, across model classes, prediction tasks, and distribution shifts.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [Randomized Sketching is Robust to Low-Precision Rounding on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20195)
*Aryaman Jeendgar,Clément Flint,Hartwig Anzt*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对GPU加速的混合精度稀疏草图算法，实验表明FP16 SparseStack在多种舍入策略下均能保持高质量的子空间嵌入，其中确定性舍入因高效性成为最佳选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU上稀疏草图（Sparse Sketching）的性能瓶颈在于内存访问和原子操作积累，而非浮点计算能力。引入低精度计算（混合精度）有望缓解这一瓶颈，但需验证低精度累加是否会损害嵌入质量。

Method: 在GPU上实现并评估了SparseStack的多种FP16变体（包括确定性取整、精确随机取整和抖动取整），并与FP32 SparseStack、CountSketch、混合精度CountSketch及FlashSketch进行了对比测试。

Result: FP16 SparseStack在不同舍入规则下表现出几乎一致的子空间畸变和最小二乘解精度。对于相干（coherent）输入，SparseStack显著优于CountSketch，而在非相干（incoherent）输入上各方法表现相近。确定性舍入在所有FP16实现中提供了最优的性能-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 在FP16 SparseStack中，舍入规则对嵌入质量影响微乎其微，确定性舍入（deterministic rounding）因性能开销最低，在精度与性能之间取得了最佳平衡。算法本身的采样分布（如SparseStack对相干数据的优势）比量化方法更能决定最终的计算精度。

Abstract: Randomized sketching is a core primitive in randomized numerical linear algebra. On modern hardware architectures, in particular on GPUs, the performance of sparse sketches is limited by memory traffic and atomic accumulation rather than floating-point throughput. This makes sketching a natural target for mixed precision, provided that low-precision accumulation does not degrade the embedding quality.
  We study mixed-precision GPU implementations of sparse oblivious subspace embeddings, focusing on a SparseStack generalization of the GPU CountSketch kernel of Higgins et al. SparseStack improves embedding quality relative to CountSketch on coherent inputs, but its additional nonzeros per column increase atomic-update contention and reduce throughput. We therefore implement FP16 SparseStack variants using deterministic round-to-nearest, exact stochastic rounding, and dithered rounding, and compare them with FP32 SparseStack, CountSketch, mixed-precision CountSketch, and FlashSketch.
  Our main empirical finding is that, for the tested regimes, SparseStack embedding quality is insensitive to the FP16 rounding rule. Deterministic, stochastic, and dithered rounding FP16 SparseStack produce nearly identical subspace distortion and sketch-and-solve least-squares accuracy across incoherent, coherent, and adversarial test problems. The dominant accuracy factor is the sketch distribution rather than the quantization rule: SparseStack variants substantially improve distortion on coherent inputs, while all methods behave similarly on incoherent inputs. Since deterministic rounding has the lowest overhead, it provides the best performance--accuracy tradeoff among the FP16 SparseStack variants.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [Computational Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19361)
*Lucius E. J. Bynum,Rajesh Ranganath,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的“计算可识别性”框架，用 finite-sample 搜索程序替代传统的渐近理论定义，从而解决了复杂约束条件下（小样本、混合数据等）因果效应识别的实际问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果识别理论多基于无限数据、渐近理想状态的“理论可识别性”，难以应对现实中有限样本、数据噪声、复杂多变的干预/观测数据混合等实际计算及数据约束场景。

Method: 提出“计算可识别性”（computational identifiability）框架：将可识别性定义为在给定的计算搜索过程及先验分布约束下，能否在有限数据和误差容忍度内找到估计量。通过实验构建计算搜索过程，实证分析小样本、模糊图形判据、观测-干预混合数据及反事实估计等场景。

Result: 实验证明该框架能够有效处理小样本识别、模糊图形判据、混合数据类型及反事实估计等具有挑战性的任务，为因果推断提供了更具操作性的实证工具。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了“计算可识别性”框架，认为在有限样本和实际计算约束下，考察因果效应的可识别性比传统的渐近理想化定义更具实用价值，通过实验验证了该方法在处理复杂、非理想条件下的可行性。

Abstract: Identification conditions describe the computability of a target query or parameter of interest as a function of the type and amount of information available. In causal identification, this information is often expressed in the form of a causal graph, and data are observed or collected for some subset of variables in the graph. Target queries may be for a single effect alone or for a class of effects in a given model. The derivation of an identification algorithm then defines mathematically the process by which the desired causal effect(s) can be uniquely determined, theoretically, in expectation. Identifiability in expectation, or 'theoretical identifiability,' generally assumes asymptotic properties, infinite data, or other mathematically idealized conditions. In this paper, we explore a fundamental distinction between this theoretical, idealized notion of identifiability and a proposed alternative that is computation-bound. The framework we propose - 'computational identifiability' - is to instead define a finite computational search procedure for an empirical estimator. If this process finds an estimator empirically, within a desired error tolerance, then identifiability is satisfied, conditional on the specified assumptions of the search (i.e., a prior distribution over the parameters) and conditional on the search procedure itself. Through several experiments, we demonstrate how this framework allows us to answer fine-grained, practical identification questions, such as identification with small finite samples, with ambiguous graphical criteria, with mixed observational-interventional data, and across counterfactual data and estimands. Code is available at https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify.

</details>


### [241] [UltraQuant: 4-bit KV Caching for Context-Heavy Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20474)
*Inesh Chakrabarti,David Limpus,Aditi Ghai Rana,Bowen Bao,Spandan Tiwari,Thiago Crepaldi,Ashish Sirasao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UltraQuant 通过 4-bit KV 缓存量化及其在 AMD GPU 上的深度优化内核，在保证任务质量的前提下，大幅提升了智能体任务的并发吞吐能力并降低了推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文密集型智能体应用对 KV 缓存造成了巨大压力（长前缀复用和高并发需求），现有的 FP8 缓存方案难以兼顾高吞吐量与低延迟。

Method: 提出 UltraQuant 量化系统：使用 Walsh-Hadamard 旋转、非对称 K/V 处理、移除 QJL 变换并结合块级尺度（block-scale）；在 AMD GPU 上针对 CDNA4 架构开发了优化的 decode-attention 算子和支持原生 scaled-MFMA 的 FP4 推理路径。

Result: 在长上下文多轮智能体任务中，相比 FP8 KV 缓存基准，将 P50 首字延迟（TTFT）降低了 3.47 倍（全流程平均 2.3 倍），输出吞吐量提升了 1.63 倍。

Conclusion: UltraQuant 通过优化 AMD GPU 上的 4-bit KV 缓存推理，显著提升了多轮智能体工作负载的性能，证明了在保持任务质量的同时通过量化权衡来提升 GPU 利用率和降低延迟的可行性。

Abstract: Context-heavy agents place unusual pressure on the key-value (KV) cache: long prefixes are reused across many short turns, while concurrency determines whether the serving system can keep GPUs utilized. We study 4-bit KV-cache compression for this setting, using TurboQuant-style rotation and codebook quantization as a quality anchor and vLLM FP8 KV caching as the deployment anchor. We report three contributions. First, we frame 4-bit KV caching around multi-round agent workloads where task quality, cache residency, and serving throughput must be measured jointly. Second, we describe the practical design choices needed to make the 4-bit path robust, including asymmetric K/V treatment, Walsh-Hadamard rotation, QJL removal, and block-scale variants. Third, we present serving optimizations on AMD GPUs, including optimized decode-attention kernels and UltraQuant, an FP4 approximation path that uses FP8 queries, FP4 KV tensors, UE8M0 group scales, and native scaled-MFMA support on CDNA4. On a long-context, multi-turn agentic workload, UltraQuant cuts P50 time-to-first-token by 3.47x in the cache-pressured late rounds (2.3x across all rounds) and raises output throughput by 1.63x over the FP8 KV baseline.

</details>


### [242] [When to Trust, How to Distill: Multi-Foundation Model Guidance for Lightweight, Robust Scientific Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19363)
*Rupasree Dey,Abdul Matin,Nathan Orwick,Yao Zhang,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对时间序列基础模型在科学应用中存在的分布偏移和计算成本高昂问题，本文提出了 GUARD 框架。该方法通过动态路由和不确定性感知机制，实现了从多个异构教师模型到轻量级预测器的有效知识蒸馏，成功在资源受限的环境下实现了高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）直接应用于特定科学领域时存在分布式偏移（distributional misalignment），且因计算成本过高难以在边缘计算传感网络中部署。本研究旨在解决如何从这些失配的基础模型中提取结构化知识，以训练轻量化、专门化的预测器。

Method: 提出了一种基于“门控不确定性感知路由”（Guard）的知识蒸馏框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 上下文路由器（Contextual Router）：根据输入数据的局部统计特征动态选择最相关的教师模型；(2) 不确定性门控温度机制（Uncertainty-Gated Temperature）：当教师模型预测置信度与领域事实不符时，自动调节并弱化蒸馏压力。

Result: 在四个关键气候领域（气象、碳通量、土壤湿度、能源电网）的测试中，GUARD 显著降低了均方根误差（RMSE）；即便教师模型零样本准确率不佳，该方法在 28.5% 的高难度实例中仍优于全局表现最好的基础模型。

Conclusion: GUARD 框架证明了即使是存在分布偏移的预训练基础模型，依然能够为边缘计算场景下的轻量级专业模型提供关键的知识蒸馏，从而平衡了模型精度与计算资源限制之间的矛盾。

Abstract: The deployment of Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) in physical sciences is hindered by a critical trade-off: while these models encode rich, universal temporal dynamics, they suffer from severe distributional misalignment when applied zero-shot to specific scientific domains, and their computational cost prohibits deployment in edge-computing sensor networks. We address a fundamental challenge: How can we extract latent structural knowledge from misaligned foundation models (FM) to train lightweight, specialized forecasters? We propose Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard), a novel framework that reframes multiteacher distillation as an instance-wise decision process with two adaptive mechanisms: (1) a Contextual Router that dynamically selects the most relevant teacher based on local input statistics, exploiting complementarity across diverse foundation models; and (2) an Uncertainty-Gated Temperature mechanism that acts as a "circuit-breaker," automatically attenuating distillation strength when teacher confidence diverges from domain reality. We evaluate our proposed lightweight framework on four climate-critical domains: meteorology, ecosystem carbon flux, soil moisture, and energy grids. Our method significantly reduces RMSE relative to a fixed-weight multi-teacher distillation baseline, successfully distilling knowledge from pretrained FMs (teachers) even when they exhibit suboptimal zero-shot accuracy due to distribution shift between the original and target data domains. We demonstrate that these domain-misaligned teachers can still serve as critical correctives, outperforming the globally superior FMs on 28.5% of the hardest instances. Ultimately, this enables high-precision scientific forecasting suitable for resource-constrained edge deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026.

</details>


### [243] [Closing the Social-Semantic Gap: SPSD for Edge-Based Prompt Compression in Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19364)
*Abhinit Sen,Ajeet Kumar,Manaranjan Pradhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 推理预填充阶段的冗余社会用语，本文提出了 SPSD 方案，通过边缘侧小模型预先压缩提示词，在保证云端回复质量的同时，显著降低了 Token 消耗和物理能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理的预填充（Prefill）阶段是云端能耗的主要来源。研究发现许多用户提示词包含大量低信息量的“社会衔接”内容（即“社会-语义鸿沟”），导致不必要的计算成本。

Method: 提出了一种名为 SPSD (Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation) 的方案：利用 4-bit 量化的小语言模型 (SLM, 如 Gemma-2-2B) 在本地端对用户提示词进行压缩，去除冗余的“社会脚手架”语言（如礼貌用语、重复表达），保留核心语义与情感，随后再将压缩后的摘要发送至云端 LLM 进行推理。此外，引入了基于规则的门控机制以避开安全敏感领域的请求。

Result: 在 248 个提示词测试集上，该方法平均每次调用节省 99.9 个输入 Token；盲测评分显示其响应质量具有非劣效性（43% 平局，28% 蒸馏版胜出，29% 原版胜出）；预计每次调用可节省 70-270 uWh 的能耗。

Conclusion: SPSD 证明了在边缘端进行提示词语义蒸馏（Prompt Distillation）是可行的，能够在保证响应质量（非劣效性）的前提下，有效降低云端大型语言模型（LLM）的输入 Token 成本与能耗，为边缘-云端协同推理提供了高效的优化路径。

Abstract: The prefill stage of Large Language Model (LLM) inference is a growing contributor to cloud-scale energy cost. Many consumer-support and conversational prompts contain social scaffolding: politeness markers, apologetic preamble, repetition, and rapport-building language that is important for human communication but carries low marginal information for machine reasoning. We call this discrepancy the Social-Semantic Gap. We present SPSD (Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation), an edge-based pipeline that compresses user prompts using a 4-bit quantised Small Language Model before transmission to a cloud-deployed LLM. Evaluation on a 248-prompt corpus using Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M) as the SLM and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct as the cloud evaluation model yields a mean input token saving of 99.9 tokens per distilled call, with all 146 distilled calls yielding positive savings. Response quality, assessed by blind LLM-as-judge scoring across 121 pairs, is non-inferior to the raw path within a pre-specified 1-point margin on a 15-point rubric; the judge awarded 43 percent ties, 28 percent distilled wins, and 29 percent raw wins. Cosine similarity is mixed: mean 0.682, median 0.712, with 54.1 percent of pairs above the 0.70 reference threshold. Safety-critical domains are conservatively routed to passthrough via rule-based gates. Per-call net energy saving is estimated at 70-270 uWh under stated assumptions. SPSD shows that on-device prompt distillation can reduce cloud LLM input-token cost while preserving response quality within a practical non-inferiority margin.

</details>


### [244] [Performance Analysis and Optimization of 3D Generative Diffusion Models across GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19365)
*Jeeho Ryoo,Yongchan Jung,Muhammad Ali Khaliq,Weidong Zhang,Jiatong Han,Byeong Kil Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析并解决了 3D MRI 扩散模型在 GPU 训练中的计算瓶颈，通过架构感知优化（TF32 与通道布局调整）大幅提升了硬件效率，且不影响合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型（Med-DDPM）用于 3D MRI 合成时计算开销巨大（数百次 U-Net 推理），且在 GPU 上存在内存访问效率低、内核异构、Tensor Core 利用率不足等问题。

Method: 跨三代 NVIDIA GPU 对 Med-DDPM 进行深度性能分析（内核级分析、指令混合、内存访问、Warp 活动及分析器优先级评分），并引入 TF32 Tensor Core 和 3D channels-last 布局进行优化。

Result: SM 周期减少高达 100 倍，动态指令减少 100 倍，Tensor Core 利用率提升约 7 倍（1.45% 到 9.98%），IPC 提升 7%。

Conclusion: 通过针对性的架构感知优化（TF32 Tensor Core 激活与 3D channels-last 布局），显著降低了 SM 周期和动态指令数，显著提升了 Tensor Core 利用率和指令吞吐（IPC），且未损害图像合成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become essential for high-fidelity 3D MRI synthesis, yet their deployment remains constrained by substantial GPU resource demands arising from hundreds of U-Net evaluations per sample and a highly heterogeneous kernel behavior. This paper performs a comprehensive performance analysis of the state-of-the-art medical diffusion model, Med-DDPM, across three generations of NVIDIA architectures to study kernel-level runtime breakdowns, instruction-mix characteristics, memory system utilization, warp-level activities, and profiler priority-score estimates. We show that training is overwhelmingly dominated by cuDNN convolution and implicit-GEMM kernels, with inefficiencies arising from memory-access patterns, tensor-layout conversions, and limited Tensor Core utilization. Guided by these insights, we evaluate two architecture-aware optimizations TF32 Tensor Core activation and a 3D channels-last layout and demonstrate that they reduce SM cycles by up to 100x, cut dynamic instructions by 100x, raise Tensor Core utilization from 1.45 to 9.98x, and increase IPC by 7% on A100, all without degrading synthesis quality.

</details>


### [245] [Low-Energy Reduced RISC-V Instruction Subset Processor for Tsetlin Machine Inference at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19964)
*Chanda Gupta,Sanidhya Bhatia,Shaurya Priyadarshi,Himani Panwar,Rishad Shafik,Sudip Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Tsetlin Machine推理定制的RISC-V处理器架构，通过精简指令集与硬件优化，在保持可编程性的同时，显著提升了边缘AI环境下的执行效率与能效表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Tsetlin Machine专用硬件加速器多依赖于紧耦合接口和微代码编程，限制了系统的可编程性与开发灵活性；旨在实现一种既保留编程灵活性又能显著提高边缘端TM推理性能与能效的设计。

Method: 利用RISC-V的模块化特性，设计了一种针对TM推理优化的精简指令集处理器；通过指令分析（Instruction Profiling）进行指令集裁剪，并对数据路径和控制路径进行针对性简化设计。

Result: 与二值神经网络（BNN）相比，TM模型精度更高（如CIFAR-2数据集上达到88.18%对60.0%）；所提出的架构在多个数据集上将执行时间缩短了98%，平均能耗降低了29.7倍。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的专用RISC-V处理器架构通过指令集精简与数据路径优化，成功解决了Tsetlin Machine（TM）在边缘AI应用中灵活性与能效之间的矛盾，为实现可编程且高效的边缘AI系统提供了一种有效的硬件设计方案。

Abstract: Tsetlin Machine (TM) is a logic-based machine learning approach that relies on simple bitwise operations and finite-state automata, which makes it attractive for edge AI deployments. Recent work has focused on co-processor and accelerator designs based on Tsetlin Machines (TMs). Although these designs achieve high performance, they typically depend on tightly coupled interfaces, microcode-style programming, and external host processors, limiting flexibility and ease of programming. In this work, we present a domain-specific RISC-V microprocessor architecture and design flow tailored for TM inference. Leveraging the modular structure of RISC-V, we design a reduced instruction subset processor that retains programmability while targeting improved performance and lower energy consumption for TM workloads. Instruction profiling is employed to guide instruction reduction, followed by datapath and control path simplifications tailored to TM inference. Both the baseline RV32IM core and the proposed reduced core are evaluated across multiple datasets and compared with Binarized Neural Networks (BNNs), which serve as a hardware-efficient baseline due to their reliance on bitwise operations during inference. Results show that TM achieves comparable or higher accuracy (e.g., up to 88.18% on CIFAR-2 compared to 60.0% for BNN) while reducing execution time by up to 98% across multiple datasets. Furthermore, the proposed design achieves an average $29.7\times$ reduction in energy consumption, demonstrating its effectiveness for programmable and efficient edge AI systems.

</details>


### [246] [Information Lattice Learning as Probabilistic Graphical Model Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19366)
*Haizi Yu,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了信息格学习（ILL）与概率图模型之间的深刻联系，证明了 ILL 可视为一种在抽象空间学习可解释约束因子图的方法，不仅桥接了符号化抽象与概率推理，还通过最大熵原理提供了稳健的分布重构机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索信息格学习（ILL）在概率分布场景下的理论基础，特别是如何将 ILL 学习到的规则解释为概率图模型（PGM）中的边缘约束。

Method: 通过在分区格上进行交替投影（降维）和提升（升维/重构），将信号投影至抽象层级，并利用 L2 均匀性或 Shannon 熵原则进行分布重建，从而学习可解释的概率规则。

Result: 证明了 ILL 学习的规则等价于商变量上的边缘约束；展示了在 Shannon 熵下，ILL 可重构为对数线性因子图；明确了信息格的层级结构（细化/粗化）与归纳偏置的区别。

Conclusion: ILL 是针对商变量的可解释约束因子图学习框架。该研究确立了 ILL 与概率图模型（PGM）及最大熵模型的理论联系，为其在推断、可识别性及符号-概率混合学习领域的发展提供了新方向。

Abstract: Information lattice learning (ILL) learns interpretable rules of a signal by alternately projecting the signal onto a partition lattice that encodes a hierarchy of abstractions and lifting selected rules back to the signal domain. When the signal is a probability mass function, we show the probabilistic rules learned by ILL admit a natural probabilistic graphical model (PGM) interpretation and develop this interpretation in detail. A partition in ILL induces a deterministic quotient variable, and a rule is the marginal law of that quotient variable. A rule set is therefore a collection of marginal constraints over interpretable abstractions. General lifting is the feasible family of all joint distributions satisfying those constraints, while special lifting chooses a maximum-ignorance reconstruction, implemented in ILL by an L2 uniformity principle closely related to maximum entropy. Under a Shannon-entropy lifting, the same constraints yield a log-linear factor graph whose factors are indexed by learned abstractions. The information lattice itself, however, is not a Bayesian network: its edges encode refinement and coarsening of abstractions, not conditional dependence. Thus ILL is best viewed as structure learning for interpretable constraint-based factor graphs over quotient variables. This view clarifies how ILL relates to graphical models and maximum entropy models, while suggesting new directions for inference, identifiability, and hybrid symbolic-probabilistic learning.

</details>


### [247] [Weibull Weight-Scale Parameter Evolution under AdamW Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19367)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过“三力分解”模型揭示了AdamW训练中权重尺度参数λ演变的动力学机制，指出对齐力是推动权重增长的核心因素，并提出了一种从检查点精确重构该力量的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究尚未深入探讨AdamW优化器中权重分布尺度参数λ在训练过程中表现出的先增长、后超调、再弛豫的非线性动力学行为。

Method: 1. 基于二参数Weibull框架分析变压器权重分布；2. 推导AdamW更新中平方权重范数的“三力分解”模型（对齐力、注入力、衰减力）；3. 在Pythia-70M模型上进行实证分析；4. 引入样条位移法（spline displacement method）从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力。

Result: 1. 对齐力在权重增长阶段占据主导地位（贡献88-94%的力预算）；2. 饱和阶段是对齐力与衰减力平衡的体现；3. 样条位移法在恢复对齐力方面达到92-94%的准确率；4. 权重增长表现出数据依赖性。

Conclusion: AdamW训练中权重尺度参数λ的演变是由对齐力（alignment force）、注入力（injection force）和衰减力（decay force）这三种力量的动力学平衡决定的。权重增长主要由对齐力驱动，而饱和与弛豫则是对齐力与权重衰减力达到平衡的结果。此外，研究提出的样条位移法能有效估计缺乏优化器动量时的对齐力。

Abstract: Building on a two-parameter Weibull framework for diagnosing transformer weight distributions, we study why the Weibull weight-scale parameter $λ$ grows, overshoots, and then relaxes during AdamW training. We derive a leading-order three-force decomposition of the squared weight norm from the AdamW update: an alignment force measuring the correlation between weights and the adaptive update direction, an injection force from adaptive step magnitude, and a decay force from decoupled weight decay. On self-trained Pythia-70M models with ground-truth optimizer moments, alignment dominates the rise phase, contributing 88-94% of the absolute force budget across four random seeds and remaining robust to super-weight removal. Near saturation, alignment and decay approach balance, explaining the transition from weight-scale growth to relaxation. These force dynamics directly govern the squared-norm component underlying $λ(t)$; the remaining RMS-to-Weibull reconstruction offset is measurable and decomposes into bridge and integration components, totaling approximately 5-6% in densely sampled regions. To extend the analysis to real models where optimizer moments are unavailable, we introduce a spline displacement method that recovers the alignment force from sparse checkpoints with approximately 92-94% accuracy, about twice the naive two-point baseline. We further observe that the peak value of $λ(t)$ varies with training-data coherence in our experiments, suggesting a data-dependent component of weight-scale growth that we leave to a controlled follow-up study. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public.

</details>


### [248] [Zero-Inflated Gaussian Distributions Enable Parameter-Space Sparsity in Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19369)
*Andreas Faust,Sven Nitzsche,Juergen Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对稀疏黑盒优化问题，本文提出了一种基于多变量零膨胀高斯分布的分布估计算法（ZIG-EDA），实现了稀疏模式与活跃参数的联合优化，有效避免了手工算子设计，并在稀疏性与算法性能上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的分布估计算法（EDAs）在连续空间表现优异，但在处理稀疏空间问题时，现有方法被迫重新引入手工设计的算子或复杂的双层方案，违背了EDA避免人工设计算子的初衷。作者旨在填补这一空白，提出一种无需手动干预的稀疏EDA方法。

Method: 提出了一种新的多变量零膨胀高斯（ZIG）分布作为EDA的采样定律。利用潜在高斯模型（latent Gaussian model）通过指标维度和数值维度分别建模稀疏模式与活跃参数，实现两者的联合优化；同时，引入了基于摊销反演（amortized inversion-based）的参数估计器。

Result: 在Lunar Lander基准测试中，ZIG-EDA在收敛速度和最终收益上均优于稠密高斯EDA、手工定制稀疏进化算法及特定的稀疏EDA，并能以极少数活跃参数构建出高质量的控制器。

Conclusion: 作者通过提出基于多变量零膨胀高斯分布（ZIG）的EDA，成功统一了稀疏模式与活跃参数的优化过程。该方法摆脱了对手工稀疏算子的依赖，为稀疏空间的黑盒优化提供了更具通用性和有效性的解决方案。

Abstract: Estimation-of-distribution algorithms (EDAs) are a powerful class of evolutionary methods for black-box optimization, especially when little is known about the structure of the objective. Whereas classical evolutionary algorithms rely on hand-designed mutation and crossover operators, hard to devise for unknown problem structures, and a source of bias, EDAs sidestep operator design entirely: they fit a probability distribution to the best individuals and sample the next generation from it. EDAs are well established on continuous parameter spaces, but they have not previously been generalized to sparse ones, in which most coefficients of a good solution are exactly zero. Existing sparse black-box optimizers therefore reintroduce exactly what EDAs were designed to avoid: hand-crafted sparsity operators, bi-level schemes alternating between support set and active values, zeroing thresholds, and other baked-in assumptions. We close this gap by proposing multivariate zero-inflated Gaussian (ZIG) distributions as EDA sampling laws. A latent Gaussian model with separate indicator and value dimensions represents sparsity patterns, correlations among active parameters, and the interactions between the two, so sparsity patterns and active values are optimized jointly, hierarchy-free. We show that the latent parameters of this model are identifiable from observed samples, unlike in the missing-data settings where related constructions originate, and introduce practical amortized inversion-based estimators for them. The estimators accurately recover latent correlation structures, and on the Lunar Lander benchmark the resulting ZIG-EDA converges faster and reaches higher final returns than a dense Gaussian EDA, a hand-crafted sparse evolutionary algorithm, and an ad-hoc sparse EDA, while finding controllers with only a small fraction of parameters active.

</details>


### [249] [Human-like autonomy emerges from self-play and a pinch of human data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19370)
*Daphne Cornelisse,Julian Hunt,Zixu Zhang,Waël Doulazmi,Kevin Joseph,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Eugene Vinitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对自我博弈驾驶策略缺乏人类兼容性的问题，提出“Spiced Self-play”：通过引入极少量（30分钟）人类数据作为正则项，平衡博弈策略与人类习惯，显著降低了数据依赖和算力需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯自我博弈训练出的驾驶策略往往会产生不符合人类驾驶习惯的“异类”行为，而传统解决方法（如奖励工程和域随机化）既复杂又脆弱。

Method: 提出了一种结合自我博弈（Self-play）与少量人类演示（仅需30分钟，作为正则化目标而非学习主体）的方法，配合最小化安全目标达成奖励进行训练。

Result: 相比同类模仿学习方法，该方法在减少2500倍人类数据需求的情况下，依然能实现与人类轨迹良好的协作，且能在消费级GPU上以15小时完成训练。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，在自我博弈强化学习中引入少量高质量人类演示作为正则化目标，能够有效解决“驱动策略与人类行为不兼容”的问题，且具有极高的样本效率和计算效率。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning has recently emerged as a way to train driving policies without any human data. It uses cheap, large-scale simulations to substitute expensive, large-scale human driving demonstrations. A key limitation of this approach is that policies trained through pure self-play can learn effective but alien driving conventions incompatible with people. Previous works attempt to mitigate such behavioral misalignments through extensive reward engineering and domain randomization, which are brittle and labor-intensive. Instead of completely discarding human demonstrations, our method treats them as a regularization objective on top of a minimal safe goal-reaching reward. Like the spice in a good stew, we find that a little human data goes a long way: our method uses only 30 minutes of human demonstrations, 2500x fewer than comparable imitation learning approaches. Resulting policies coordinate with held-out human trajectories and complete training in 15 hours on a single consumer-grade GPU. Videos and full source code are available at https://spiced-self-play.com/.

</details>


### [250] [Data Bias Mitigation under Coverage Constraints & The Price of Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20461)
*Bruno Scarone,Alfredo Viola,Renée J. Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过整数线性规划引入覆盖约束的偏差缓解框架，在解决交叉子群偏见问题的同时，量化并优化了公平性代价与数据处理成本之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在处理交叉敏感属性（如种族与性别）时表现出偏见，主要源于缺乏有效的量化指标以及交叉子群在训练数据中代表性不足。

Method: 提出了一种扩展的偏差缓解框架，引入覆盖约束（Coverage Constraints）以保证交叉子群的代表性；将偏差缓解建模为整数线性规划（ILP）问题，并量化了公平性代价与数据修改成本之间的函数关系。

Result: 实验表明该方法在保持模型预测性能的同时，成功降低了交叉群体的偏差，且覆盖约束对于维持下游任务的机器学习表现至关重要。

Conclusion: 该研究通过在偏差缓解中引入覆盖约束，证明了在保留预测准确性的同时，能够有效解决交叉群体偏差问题，并为公平性与数据效率之间的权衡提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Machine learning models have been shown to exhibit discriminatory outcomes or degraded performance for individuals at the intersection of multiple sensitive attributes, such as race and gender. This stems in part from two interrelated challenges: the lack of principled measures for quantifying bias (potentially intersectional), and insufficient representation of intersectional subgroups in training data. We extend a recent bias mitigation framework to incorporate coverage constraints that enforce sufficient representation across groups, including intersectional subgroups. Since achieving exactly zero bias for all groups may not be data efficient (meaning it may require large amounts of data), our solution trades small approximation errors in bias for greater data efficiency while satisfying coverage constraints. We also formulate bias mitigation as an integer linear program that optimizes over all mitigation strategies, and characterize the price of fairness, the minimum data modification cost, as a function of fairness tolerance. This is essential both for legal compliance, where regulations may mandate specific fairness thresholds, and for data governance, enabling practitioners to make informed trade-offs between bias reduction and data modification (particularly, data purchasing) costs. We evaluate our techniques on publicly available datasets, demonstrating that bias mitigation via our framework preserves predictive accuracy across multiple classifiers, and that coverage constraints, while motivated by statistical considerations, are essential for preserving downstream ML performance.

</details>


### [251] [ProMUSE: Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty-guided Staged Evidential Alzheimer Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19371)
*Long Doan,Branden Chen,Ethan Litton,Huan Huang,Jiajing Huang,Yixin Xie,Weihua Zhou,Nandakumar Narayanan,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProMUSE是一种按需引入成本高昂影像数据的AI诊断框架，通过不确定性驱动的渐进式融合策略，在极大地节约医疗成本的同时，实现了高效的阿尔茨海默病早期诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病早期诊断至关重要，但依赖MRI和PET等昂贵成像导致临床应用受限。现有方法往往要求全模态输入，缺乏经济效率。

Method: 提出了一种渐进式多模态不确定性引导分阶段证据网络（ProMUSE）。该模型基于狄利克雷主观逻辑量化临床数据的不确定性，并利用Dempster-Shafer理论融合多模态置信度。模型采用分阶段策略，仅在不确定性超过阈值时才引入高成本的MRI或PET影像数据。

Result: 在ADNI、AIBL和OASIS数据集的多个分类任务中，ProMUSE在达到与全模态基准线相当甚至更好准确率的同时，减少了50%-90%的MRI/PET使用量。

Conclusion: ProMUSE 为阿尔茨海默病（AD）诊断提供了一种实用、具有不确定性感知且资源高效的解决方案，在大幅降低昂贵影像检查需求的同时，保持了高诊断准确性。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a fatal disorder that destroys memory and cognitive skills in the elderly population. Most treatments for AD are effective in the early stage, leading to an increasing demand for early AD diagnosis. AD diagnosis increasingly relies on multimodal data such as clinical assessments, structural Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI), and Positron Emission Tomography (PET) imaging. However, MRI and PET acquisition remain costly and not universally accessible, making full-modality inference impractical in real-world clinical workflows. We propose ProMUSE, a Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty Guided Staged Evidential Network that adaptively determines when additional modalities are necessary, helping reduce the overall cost of data acquisition while maintaining accuracy. ProMUSE first performs evidential classification using low-cost clinical data and quantifies uncertainty via a Dirichlet-based subjective logic model. When uncertainty exceeds a learned threshold, ProMUSE progressively incorporates MRI or PET features, fusing modality-wise belief and uncertainty through Dempster-Shafer theory to obtain a calibrated multimodal prediction. This staged acquisition strategy enables accurate diagnosis while minimizing reliance on expensive imaging. Experiments on ADNI, AIBL, and OASIS across CN-AD, CN-MCI, and MCI-AD tasks demonstrate that ProMUSE achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared to full-modality baselines while reducing MRI/PET usage by 50-90%, yielding substantial cost savings. These results highlight ProMUSE as a practical, uncertainty-aware, and resource-efficient solution for real-world AD screening.

</details>


### [252] [cAPM: Continual AI-Assisted Pace-Mapping with Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19373)
*Dylan O'Hara,Pradeep Bajracharya,Casey Meisenzahl,Karli Gillette,Anton J. Prassl,Gernot Plank,Saman Nazarian,Roderick Tung,John L Sapp,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: cAPM 是一种新型持续学习 AI 系统，通过迁移既往起搏标测经验，显著减少了室性心动过速介入消融所需的起搏点数量并提升了定位准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的用于引导 VT 消融的 AI 起搏标测方法缺乏跨目标或跨患者的知识迁移能力，需要为每个新的 VT 目标重新训练，限制了临床效率。

Method: 提出了一种名为 cAPM 的连续人工智能辅助起搏标测方法。该方法结合了三个核心组件：（1）学习起搏位点到 12 导联心电图形态映射的与任务无关的代理神经网络；（2）选择最信息量起搏位点的主动学习策略；（3）能够在保留先验目标知识的同时顺序处理新目标的持续学习策略。

Result: 在模拟测试中，cAPM 在使用 4.5 个起搏位点的情况下，达到了 81% 的 5mm 精度定位成功率；相比之下，现有的主动学习方法在使用 13.7 个位点时成功率仅为 38%。

Conclusion: cAPM 框架通过利用连续学习和知识迁移，显著提高了室性心动过速（VT）起搏标测（pace-mapping）的效率和准确性，为临床应用提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Ventricular tachycardia is a life-threatening rhythm disorder and a major cause of sudden cardiac death. Pace-mapping is a clinical procedure for identifying the intervention target during catheter ablation of VT. It requires clinicians to pace different sites in the ventricles and rapidly interpret the resulting electrocardiograms to determine where to pace next or whether a target site has been identified. Active learning AI models have been proposed to guide clinicians to the next pacing site, showing promise in reducing the number of pacing sites and improving the efficiency of pace-mapping. Existing methods require retraining each target without the ability to transfer knowledge across multiple VTs within the same patient or across patients. We introduce cAPM for continuous AI-assisted pace-mapping to capture and transfer knowledge accumulated from past pace-mapping data to reduce the number of pace-mapping data needed for future target VTs. This is made possible by a task-agnostic surrogate neural network that learns the mapping from pacing sites to 12-lead ECG morphology, an active-learning strategy that refines this surrogate model by selecting the most informative pacing site for each target, and a continual learning strategy to do so sequentially while retaining knowledge from prior targets. Evaluated on an in-silico testbed consisting of sequentially-presented localization tasks across different physiological conditions and ventricular geometries, cAPM with and without replay of past data samples achieved an 81% probability of localizing within clinical tolerance (5 mm accuracy) using 4.5 pace-mapping sites, compared to the state-of-the-art active-learning method achieving 38% probability using 13.7 pacing sites. These results provide a strong basis for preparing cAPM towards in-vivo preclinical and clinical studies where it can be used to guide pace-mapping.

</details>


### [253] [Algebraic Dead Directions in LayerNorm Transformers: A Forward-Pass-Only Diagnostic at LLM Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19491)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar,P. J. Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LayerNorm中的缩放参数直接决定了Transformer参数空间中的“死亡方向”，无需进行任何前向或反向计算即可直接读取，为分析模型的损失曲面几何特征提供了极低成本的解析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法定位Transformer损失曲面的“死亡方向”（Fisher信息矩阵退化方向）通常需要计算复杂的激活特征分解或采样，计算开销巨大。研究旨在寻找一种仅利用网络参数本身即可直接读取这些方向的高效方法。

Method: 通过代数分析，证明了LayerNorm仿射参数的逆比例方向 $\gamma^{-1}/\|\gamma^{-1}\|$ 是后置归一化中心化激活协方差的精确代数核；通过对14个不同规模和架构的预训练Transformer模型进行实证测试（对比LayerNorm与RMSNorm），验证了该方向与模型真实“死亡方向”的一致性。

Result: 在随机初始化时，预测方向与实测奇异方向在9个LayerNorm模型中达到四位小数的一致性；在RMSNorm模型上该属性缺失；证实了该方向在训练后的鲁棒性，并揭示了残差流奇异值在层间的保留性质以及通过参数区分归一化类型的能力。

Conclusion: 研究展示了LayerNorm中的缩放参数可直接诱导模型参数空间中的“死亡方向（dead direction）”，无需进行前向或反向传播即可计算。这一发现为理解Transformer的损失曲面几何形状以及层归一化对模型结构的影响提供了新的代数解析视角。

Abstract: Pretrained transformers sit near singular minima of the loss, where the Fisher information metric degenerates along dead directions: directions in parameter space along which the directional Fisher vanishes. Locating such a direction normally needs a forward pass and an eigendecomposition of activations, or a sampling-based complexity estimate; none returns a direction computable from the network's parameters alone. We give one, for LayerNorm transformers. The inverse-scale direction $γ^{-1}/\|γ^{-1}\|$ of the LayerNorm affine is an exact algebraic kernel of the post-final-norm centred activation covariance, for any input distribution, and induces a corresponding dead direction in parameter space. It is read from the LN scale parameter alone, with no forward or backward pass and no eigensolve: the cheapest dead-direction read, specific to LayerNorm. We test it on $14$ pretrained transformers ($9$ LayerNorm, $5$ RMSNorm; $160$M-$35$B; language and vision objectives). At random initialisation the predicted direction matches the measured bottom singular direction (one forward pass, direct SVD) to four decimal places on $9/9$ LayerNorm models, and is correctly absent on $5/5$ RMSNorm models, which lack the mean-subtraction projector that creates it. On the trained checkpoint the covariance eigenvalue along this direction deepens by ${\sim}10^3\times$ and further dead directions open; the random-init-to-trained gap is a one-forward-pass, per-checkpoint readout of singular structure along the predicted coordinate. Two consequences follow in closed form: the residual stream's smallest singular value is preserved block-to-block on $13/14$ transformers measured on their own input distribution, the one exception (Gemma$4$-$31$B) a genuine dead direction the same read pinpoints; and the kernel direction's presence classifies a transformer's normalisation from the parameters alone.

</details>


### [254] [Protein Representation Learning with Secondary-Structure and Energy-Filtered Hydrogen-Bond Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19374)
*Mohamed Mouhajir,Limei Wang,El Houcine Bergou,Hajar El Hammouti,Lamiae Azizi,Dongqi Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合二级结构信息与能量筛选氢键网络的GNN模型SSProNet，有效提升了蛋白质表征的学习效果与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于序列邻接或几何距离的蛋白质图表示方法未能充分体现蛋白质折叠的物理化学准则，尤其是缺失了对蛋白质稳定性与功能至关重要的二级结构（如α-螺旋、β-折叠）及氢键相互作用的显式建模。

Method: 提出了一种二级结构感知图神经网络（Secondary-structure-aware GNN）。具体方法包括：1）在残基层面的节点表示中引入二级结构信息；2）构建基于氢键能量强度筛选的图边，以捕捉局部结构基序及长程稳定性相互作用。

Result: 在多个蛋白质基准数据集上，该方法表现优于现有的各类图神经网络方法；同时，学到的图连接模式与蛋白质中的经典结构基序高度吻合，增强了模型的生物学可解释性。

Conclusion: 将二级结构信息与基于能量筛选的氢键拓扑相结合，能够为蛋白质表征学习提供有效的归纳偏置，从而提升模型在蛋白质结构任务上的性能与生物学可解释性。

Abstract: Graph-based representations are widely used in protein modeling, yet many existing approaches rely primarily on sequence adjacency or geometric proximity, which only partially reflect the principles governing protein folding. Proteins instead adopt complex three-dimensional conformations organized around secondary structure elements, such as $α$-helices and $β$-sheets, which encode recurring local motifs and stabilizing hydrogen-bond interactions.
